## Premium Studio Shot of Blueberry Mint Iced Drink

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik ürün fotoğrafı üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "meta": {
    "title": "Blueberry Mint Iced Drink – Premium Studio Shot",
    "version": "1.0",
    "type": "text-to-image",
    "style": "Ultra-realistic beverage photography"
  },
  "scene": {
    "subject": "Tall clear highball glass filled with blue blueberry mint soda",
    "glass": "Transparent faceted glass with condensation droplets",
    "liquid": "Vibrant gradient blue drink (deep blue at bottom fading to clear at top)",
    "ice": "Large crystal-clear ice cubes filling the glass",
    "garnish": [
      "Fresh mint leaves inside and on top",
      "Whole blueberries inside the drink",
      "Blueberries resting around the base",
      "Light wooden straw angled inside the glass"
    ],
    "effects": [
      "Soft misty vapor rising from the top",
      "Tiny bubbles in liquid",
      "Water droplets on glass surface",
      "Slight splash texture at bottom surface"
    ]
  },
  "environment": {
    "background": "Dark navy to black gradient backdrop",
    "surface": "Glossy black reflective surface with scattered blueberries and mint leaves",
    "atmosphere": "Moody, cinematic, high contrast"
  },
  "lighting": {
    "type": "Professional studio lighting",
    "setup": [
      "Soft diffused key light from left",
      "Cool rim light from right to enhance glass edges",
      "Subtle backlight for vapor visibility"
    ],
    "highlights": "Sharp reflections on ice and glass",
    "shadows": "Deep, dramatic shadows"
  },
  "camera": {
    "angle": "Eye-level close-up",
    "lens": "85mm macro lens",
    "aperture": "f/2.8",
    "depth_of_field": "Shallow depth of field, blurred background berries",
    "resolution": "8K ultra-detailed",
    "focus": "Sharp focus on glass center and mint leaves"
  },
  "quality_tags": [
    "hyper-realistic",
    "photorealistic",
    "commercial beverage photography",
    "crisp details",
    "cinematic",
    "high dynamic range",
    "editorial drink advertisement style"
  ]
}
```

## Ethereal Product Visual with Frosted Glass Hands

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
 "task": "photorealistic_product_visual",
 "input": {
 "reference_image": "USER_UPLOADED_IMAGE",
 "preserve_product_identity": true,
 "preserve_label_text": true,
 "preserve_object_shape": true,
 "preserve_centered_composition": true
 },
 "scene": {
 "environment": "high-key studio",
 "background": "pure white seamless",
 "negative_space": "very high",
 "depth_of_field": "layered depth with strong subject isolation"
 },
 "subject": {
 "primary_object": "single product object",
 "position": "perfectly centered, floating",
 "orientation": "upright",
 "scale": "natural product scale",
 "clarity": "ultra-sharp, high micro-contrast"
 },
 "human_elements": {
 "hands": {
 "count": 2,
 "layering": "positioned behind a frosted glass surface",
 "visual_effect": "frosted glass diffusion, milky blur, softened edges",
 "transparency": "semi-opaque",
 "focus": "intentionally out of focus",
 "gesture": "slow, delicate, abstract reach",
 "interaction": "no contact with the product"
 }
 },
 "optical_effects": {
 "frosted_glass": {
 "applied_to": "hands only",
 "blur_type": "diffusion blur",
 "edge_softness": "high",
 "light_scatter": "subtle bloom"
 }
 },
 "lighting": {
 "style": "soft diffused studio lighting",
 "direction": "frontal with gentle top wash",
 "shadows": "extremely soft and minimal",
 "highlights": "clean, controlled highlights on the product only",
 "contrast": "low overall, high on product"
 },
 "materials_rendering": {
 "product_surface": "premium, realistic, tactile",
 "reflections": "clean and intentional",
 "imperfections": "none"
 },
 "aesthetic": {
 "mood": "ethereal, calm, refined",
 "style": "high-end editorial product photography",
 "visual_language": "minimal, airy, modern, poetic",
 "emotion": "care, purity, precision"
 },
 "camera": {
 "lens": "85mm",
 "aperture": "f/2.0",
 "angle": "eye-level",
 "camera_motion": "none"
 },
 "post_processing": {
 "retouching": "luxury commercial polish",
 "grain": "none",
 "color_grading": "clean whites with subtle warmth on the product"
 },
 "output": {
 "resolution": "ultra_high_resolution",
 "focus_priority": "product only",
 "clarity_separation": "product razor sharp, background and hands diffused",
 "artifacts": "none"
 }
}
```

## Crystal Hologram Product Photography Prompt

> Fütüristik ürün fotoğrafı üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "ultra_high_fidelity_request": {
    "subject": "{argument name="product" default="[PRODUCT]"}",
    "framing": {
      "position": "Strictly centered and levitating",
      "boundary": "Full product visibility, zero cropping, isolated in 3D space",
      "background": "Solid pure black hex #000000"
    },
    "optical_effects": {
      "iridescence": "Extremely brilliant spectral iridescence, vivid neon oil-slick gradients",
      "volumetric": "Internal volumetric lighting, ethereal inner glow, light scattering within the material",
      "refraction": "High-index caustic light refraction, prismatic color splitting at the edges",
      "luminosity": "Super-saturated holographic highlights, intense electric blues and deep magentas"
    },
    "material_finish": {
      "texture": "Ultra-glossy liquid-smooth finish",
      "base": "Deep translucent obsidian glass with infinite depth",
      "reflections": "Crisp ray-traced mirror reflections of an invisible light source"
    },
    "negative_constraints": [
      "No platforms", "No floor", "No light boxes", "No softboxes", 
      "No props", "No text", "No shadows on surfaces", "No pedestals"
    ]
  }
}
```

Ultra-realistic cinematic product photography of a luxury perfume bottle filled with deep red liquid, standing upright and centered. Thick glass bottle with sharp edges and a bold sculptural cap, premium minimal label. Surrounded by dark rugged wood textures, bark, and crushed wood chips, creating a rich woody atmosphere. Dramatic red and black background with smoky haze and glowing embers, intense contrast lighting. Strong rim light highlighting the bottle silhouette, glossy reflections on glass, deep shadows for a mysterious mood. High-end fragrance advertising style, moody, sensual, powerful aesthetic, hyper-detailed textures, ultra-sharp focus, photorealistic, 8K quality.
```

## Fluid Chrome Distortion Product Render

> Görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "product_type": "{argument name=\"product type\" default=\"[PRODUCT]\"}",
  "prompt": "A [product_type] floating against a pure black void background (#000000). The product is small and centered in frame with generous black negative space on all sides, the product occupies no more than 50% of the frame and is never cropped. The product is visualized through a liquid vertical streak effect, as if the product has been melted and stretched into smooth, flowing vertical streaks of liquid metal. The streaks are buttery smooth, fluid, and organic like mercury or molten silver flowing downward. Each streak blends and bleeds softly into the next with no hard edges, no sharp block corners, no rigid geometry. The vertical streaks have varying thickness and luminosity, creating a sense of depth and dimensionality. The liquid streaks glow with cool monochromatic {argument name=\"color tone\" default=\"blue-silver\"} iridescent tones, think liquid mercury under blue light. The streaks flow smoothly with gentle luminous gradients, bright specular highlights fading into deep shadow. The effect is contained within the product's general silhouette shape with pure black void surrounding it. The overall impression should be a product dissolving into smooth vertical streams of liquid light. No hard edges, no blocky slats, no rigid bars.",
  "negative_prompt": "hard edges, sharp corners, blocky slats, rigid bars, chrome blocks, machined metal, solid geometric shapes, cropped product, cut off edges, radial pattern, circular rings, horizontal lines, warm colors, red, orange, yellow, green, colored background, flat lighting, matte surface",
  "style_parameters": {
    "background_color": "#000000 pure black void",
    "effect": "smooth liquid vertical streaks, fluid mercury-like, buttery soft, no hard edges",
    "effect_color": "iridescent monochromatic liquid blue-silver #C0C0C0",
    "pattern_type": "flowing liquid metal vertical streaks with soft gradients — NOT rigid blocks or bars",
    "product_framing": "centered, small in frame, max 50% canvas, NEVER cropped, generous black space",
    "product_color": "liquid mercury blue-silver iridescent",
    "lighting": "smooth luminous gradients, soft specular highlights, liquid light quality"
  }
}
```

*/

{
  "ENVIRONMENT": {

  "RENDER_OUTPUT": {

}

{
  "meta": {
    "title": "Hyper-Realistic Appy Fizz Product Shot",
    "version": "1.0",
    "type": "text-to-image-prompt"
  },

    },

      "background": "seamless solid #59925C background with no floor, surface, pedestal, platform, table, shadow ground, or horizon line — the product floats against a uniform single-color field",
      "grounding": "no visible ground plane, no reflective surface beneath the product"
    },

      "finish": "polished edges with subtle holographic light dispersion",
      "hardware_detail": "macro-precision on hex-head bolt sockets",
      "layer_direction": "every glass/acrylic layer is strictly vertical — no horizontal slabs, curved shells, or molded forms",

    }
  }
}

## High-Contrast Studio Product Shot of Serum Bottle

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal ürün fotoğrafı üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
IMAGE: A single cylindrical serum bottle is positioned slightly right of center in the frame, diagonally tilted from the upper right toward the lower left, creating a bottom-weighted composition with most visual mass concentrated in the lower middle of the image; the camera presents a three-quarter front view of the bottle, with the front label and a slight portion of its right side visible, viewed from slightly above eye level and angled downward, as if the camera is hovering just above the subject; the bottle appears suspended mid-air, gripped near its neck by a black mechanical clamp that enters from the upper right corner, partially cropped by the frame edge so that the top and rightmost portions of the clamp extend beyond the border; the bottle’s cylindrical body has rounded edges and a smooth, matte label surface, while the cap is also cylindrical with a flat top, and the entire form is oriented diagonally, pointing toward the lower left corner; thick, glossy {argument name="liquid color" default="green"} liquid coats the upper portion of the bottle and cap, forming viscous drips that curve downward under gravity, with rounded edges and reflective highlights indicating a semi-transparent, gel-like texture; the clamp consists of angular, geometric components—rectangular arms and jointed segments with sharp edges—forming a rigid, industrial silhouette around the bottle’s neck, its matte black surface absorbing most of the light and appearing nearly featureless; the environment is a seamless, uniform light-grey background with no visible horizon or texture, creating a clean, studio-like setting with no foreground or background objects besides the clamp and bottle; the camera perspective feels close to medium-close distance with minimal perspective distortion, suggesting a normal to slightly telephoto focal length; focus is sharp across both the clamp and the bottle, with no noticeable depth-of-field blur, giving the image a crisp, product-catalog clarity; lighting is hard and directional from the upper left, producing bright specular highlights along the bottle’s curved surface and the glossy green liquid, while casting deep, dense shadows across the clamp, which reads as a near-black silhouette; the light creates subtle reflections on the bottle’s smooth surfaces and accentuates the thickness of the dripping liquid; effects include high contrast between the white label, black clamp, and grey background, with clean edges, minimal visible grain, and a slightly stylized color grade that emphasizes the green liquid as the only saturated accent; the color palette is restrained—neutral grey background, black clamp, white label, and a vivid green liquid—creating a cool, clinical, high-contrast atmosphere; there is no visible haze, dust, or motion blur, and the scene appears entirely static and controlled. TEXT: Title: “{argument name="product title" default="THE RETINOL SERUM"}”; Body: “TRANSFORMATIVE VITAMIN A COMPLEX”; Other: “30 ML / 1.0 FL OZ”.
```

> Minimal ürün fotoğrafı üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

IMAGE: Frame & Composition: The camera uses a low-angle, straight-on frontal view, positioned slightly below the subjects so the two cans rise above the horizon line; both cans are upright and vertically oriented, placed side by side with a narrow gap, occupying the central upper third of the frame; the lower edge crops into a metallic ledge that acts as a base plane, while the cans are fully visible from base to rim; the composition is symmetrical but slightly staggered in height, with the right can marginally taller in frame due to perspective; negative space dominates the upper background, reinforcing vertical emphasis and product isolation. Lighting & Atmosphere: A frontal key light, slightly elevated and centered, produces even illumination across both cans, generating dense, high-contrast specular highlights on condensation droplets; shadows are minimal and soft, indicating diffused fill from the front and sides; the moisture beads catch light sharply, suggesting a hard component within the otherwise controlled lighting setup; no atmospheric haze is present, but surface condensation creates a tactile, cold visual cue. Subject Geometry & Materiality: Each subject is a tall, cylindrical aluminum can with smooth vertical walls and rounded rims; both are coated in matte white printed labels with bold, high-contrast typography; circular color graphics are printed flat onto the surface—{argument name="left can color" default="magenta and red"} on the left can, {argument name="right can color" default="yellow and green"} on the right can; the aluminum surfaces are covered with uniformly distributed water droplets of varying diameters, clinging via surface tension; droplets create micro-lens effects, distorting underlying text and graphics; edges are crisp, with no deformation, indicating rigid packaging. Environmental Layering: The foreground consists of a horizontal metallic ledge with linear grooves and visible fasteners, rendered in sharp focus; the cans sit directly atop this ledge, establishing physical contact; the midground is entirely occupied by the cans; the background is a uniform, out-of-focus deep red gradient wall with subtle tonal variation and no discernible texture; no additional objects or layers are present beyond these three planes. Technical Artifacts & Color: The color palette contrasts cool whites and saturated accent colors on the cans against a warm, dark red background; saturation is moderate to high in the printed circles, while the background remains subdued; fine film grain is visible across the image, especially in the darker red areas, suggesting either high ISO or intentional texture; slight vignetting darkens the frame edges; no chromatic aberration or motion blur is visible; highlights on droplets are sharp with controlled roll-off. TEXT: Title: “IMAGE & COMMERCE”; Subtitle: “Cinematic prompt just for you” (partially visible); Body: Flavor names including “{argument name=\"left flavor\" default=\"Raspberry Guava\"}” on the left can and “Lemon Lime” on the right can; CTA: None; Logo: “I&C”; Disclaime

## High-End Concept Art Magazine Editorial Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[BRAND NAME]: 
A high-end, glossy concept art magazine editorial photograph of a unique, unexpected functional object conceptualized and designed by the brand.

**1. The Concept & Object (AI Invention):**
Based on the design philosophy, heritage, and material vocabulary of the specified brand, the AI must invent a novel utility product (NOT standard clothing, shoes, or bags). Examples could be home goods, tech accessories, tools, or sporting equipment, reinterpretated through the brand's lens. The object should feel sculptural yet functional.

**2. Materials & Details (Hyper-Premium):**
The object is constructed from ultra-premium, highly tactile materials characteristic of the brand (e.g., patinated exotic leathers, brushed aerospace-grade titanium, sculpted matte ceramics, molded carbon fiber, or technical high-fashion textiles). Every detail is hyper-realistic: visible stitching, microscopic material grain, precision engravings, and complex texture contrasts.

**3. Photography & Lighting (Cinematic Studio):**
Shot on a medium format Phase One camera with a 100mm macro lens. Extremely shallow depth of field, with sharp focus on the hero details of the object and a creamy, smooth bokeh background. The lighting is sophisticated studio softbox lighting: gentle, enveloping fill light with precise rim lighting to accentuate contours and material textures.

**4. Environment:**
A seamless, impeccably clean studio cyclorama background in a pure, ultra-light pastel tone (e.g., desaturated mint, pale blush, or off-white), free of shadows.

**5. Layout & UI Elements (Strict Placement):**
- **Bottom Right Corner:** A small, understated, monochrome gray logo of the {argument name="brand name" default="BRAND NAME"}.
- **Bottom Left Corner:** Small, minimalist monochrome gray text describing the invented product. The font style looks like Manrope Regular with very tight tracking (kerning) and balanced line spacing. Example format: "CONCEPT STUDY: {argument name="invented product name" default="[AI inserts invented product name]"}. MATERIAL: [AI inserts main materials]. SS25."
```

```
IMAGE: Frame & Composition: The camera presents a three-quarter, slightly low-angle view of a cylindrical container suspended mid-air, tilted approximately 30–40 degrees with its opening facing upward-left; the screw cap is separated and positioned above the container, offset to the right, while multiple capsules are frozen in mid-flight between the jar and the lid; the subject cluster occupies the central frame with generous negative space around it, no cropping of the primary objects, and the visual weight balanced diagonally from bottom-left (jar body) to top-right (lid). Lighting & Atmosphere: A soft but directional key light originates from the upper left, producing controlled specular highlights along the jar’s rim, capsule shells, and lid edge; shadows are soft-edged, indicating a diffused source, with subtle occlusion shadows beneath the floating capsules; there is no atmospheric haze or bloom, and contrast is moderate, preserving detail in both highlights and dark surfaces. Subject Geometry & Materiality: The main container is a matte-to-satin black cylindrical jar with a slightly translucent brown neck, showing smooth rotational symmetry and fine surface texture; the lid is a ribbed, circular disc with evenly spaced vertical grooves, suggesting injection-molded plastic; the capsules are elongated ovals with semi-opaque, fibrous interiors visible through their shells, indicating powdered contents; embossed and printed text on the jar surface is partially legible but softened by the lighting angle and material reflectance. Environmental Layering: There is no physical foreground object; the capsules occupy the immediate midground between lid and jar; the background is a continuous, seamless gradient backdrop transitioning from pale pink at the top to muted coral-red at the bottom, with no discernible depth cues or environmental elements, and no bokeh since the entire scene is uniformly in focus. Technical Artifacts & Color: The palette is restrained, dominated by black, warm gray-beige capsules, and a pink-to-red gradient background with low saturation; color transitions are smooth, with no banding visible; fine grain is present across the image, consistent with film grain or a grain overlay; no chromatic aberration, motion blur, or lens distortion is evident, and edges remain clean and precise. TEXT: Title: None; Subtitle: None; Body: “{argument name="product name" default="DEEP PUZZLE HERBAL EXTRACT"}” (legible); CTA: None; Logo: “JASPO” (embossed/printed on jar, partially legible); Disclaimer: None; Other: Additional small dosage and supplement text present but partially obscured and not fully legible.

9. Surreal elegant fusion, combining realism with imagination

Product shape, proportion, label, font, color, and branding must be 100% accurate

No distortion, deformation, or redesign

Clear separation between the product and the background

Soft, controlled studio lighting

High dynamic range, ultra-sharp focus

Editorial luxury advertising aesthetic

High-end sensory marketing look

Overall Vibe:
Modern, sophisticated, visually coherent

High-end commercial campaign

Suitable for brand websites, social grids, and digital billboards

## Jade Aesthetic Conceptual Architecture Still Life

> Minimal, lüks mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A highly detailed, exquisitely crafted conceptual product-style still life of an elegant traditional Chinese pavilion, presented as a miniature architectural artwork. The pavilion is a three-tiered pagoda structure made of semi-transparent jade-like and softly frosted glass material in refined milky white and ice-blue tones. Translucent turquoise-blue glass roofs feature delicately tapered profiles, accented with finely detailed champagne-gold ornamental edges and intricate, graceful golden flying-eave details. A warm, subtle golden light glows gently from within the pavilion through translucent windows, creating a soft inner radiance. A small vertical blue-and-gold plaque is precisely embedded on the upper tier façade, understated yet refined. The pavilion stands on a simplified, smooth sculptural base inspired by stylized mountains and calm water, carved from semi-transparent ice-blue crystal material. The mountain–water form is defined by elegant flowing contours, softly rounded transitions, and continuous sculptural surfaces, with restrained texture and no sharp ridges, evoking the feeling of finely polished jade, melted glass, or serene frozen waves. The mountain–water base fully fills the bottom of the frame and is cleanly cropped by the image edge: there is no visible ground, no platform, no horizon line, and no empty bottom space. The form appears naturally immersed and continuous, extending beyond the frame rather than resting upon it. A clean white jade staircase rises diagonally and seamlessly from within the sculptural base toward the pavilion entrance, maintaining clarity, balance, and an elegant sense of progression. Composition is tight, refined, and immersive: the pavilion and its base occupy most of the frame, with the mountain–water structure gently pressing against the lower edge, eliminating visual emptiness while preserving calmness. Camera: subtle low-angle, 3/4 perspective (approximately 25–30 degrees turned), with close framing to emphasize fine material quality, delicate craftsmanship, and sculptural precision. Soft, diffused luxury studio lighting with minimal hard shadows. High realism, shallow depth of field. Clean white to light-gray gradient background confined to the upper area, with carefully restrained negative space. Calm, ethereal, refined, premium craftsmanship, serene and sacred atmosphere, Eastern fantasy meets modern minimalism.
```

> Minimal logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
1) Vertical studio product photography of the chocolate-dusted donut based on the upload reference photo of the product, centered on a matte, circular podium painted in a warm cocoa shade, maintaining the exact logo, text, and shape of the original product. The composition strictly adheres to a monochromatic "tone-on-tone" aesthetic, where the background, surface, and props utilize varying shades of brown—from deep espresso to light latte—to create a sophisticated, layered visual experience. The donut acts as the focal point, with the dusty texture of the topping providing a tactile contrast to the smooth, minimal surroundings.
The lighting is designed to be soft yet directional, utilizing a large diffuser to wrap light around the curves of the donut while maintaining subtle shadows that define its volume. The background is a seamless paper backdrop in a rich mocha tone, creating a distraction-free environment that blends harmoniously with the product's color palette. The image is captured with a high-resolution portrait lens, ensuring crisp focus on the chocolate powder details while allowing the monochromatic colors to melt together in a creamy, cohesive commercial editorial style.

2) Vertical studio product photography of the chocolate-dusted donut based on the upload reference photo of the product, maintaining the exact shape and appearance of the original. The donut is positioned centrally, acting as the focal point amidst an intricate, multi-layered paper craft installation. Hand-cut layers of textured art paper in warm, complementary shades of mocha, latte, cream, and muted gold are stacked and arranged in organic, wave-like patterns that build up around the base of the donut, creating depth and a sophisticated, three-dimensional topographical effect.
The lighting is expertly designed using soft, directional studio lights that cast gentle, defined shadows between the paper layers, emphasizing the precise cutouts and the tactile quality of the materials. The camera angle is straight-on at eye level, capturing the rich texture of the cocoa powder against the smooth, matte finish of the paper art. The shot is framed vertically with a shallow depth of field, ensuring the donut is in razor-sharp focus while the surrounding paper layers gradually blur into an abstract, artistic backdrop, enhancing the premium, handcrafted feel of the scene.

3) Vertical studio product photography of the chocolate-dusted donut based on the upload reference photo of the product, placed centrally on a custom-made terrazzo stone surface, maintaining the exact shape and appearance of the original product. The terrazzo pattern features a stylish mix of embedded aggregate chips in muted tones of burnt orange, forest green, mustard yellow, and cream set within a warm beige base, creating a vibrant yet sophisticated textural contrast with the matte cocoa powder on the donut.
The environment is a controlled studio setting with s
```

## Image-to-SD Papercraft Conversion Prompt

> Minimal, stüdyo ışıklı görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[AI Instruction: Image → SD Papercraft Conversion]

Analyze the [Input Image].

Extraction: Read the facial features, hairstyle, hair color, clothing, accessories, and pose of the person/character from the [Input Image].
SD Conversion: Convert into a Super Deformed (SD) character papercraft, 2-3 heads tall.
Output: Generate two images.
[Image 1: Unfolded Template/Parts]
The unfolded state of the papercraft. Colorful parts printed on thick paper. Unfolded templates for the head, face, hair, torso, arms, legs, accessories, etc. Fold lines (dotted lines), glue tabs, and numbers are visible. Scissors, cutters, glue, and a cutting mat are scattered in the background. Overhead view composition. Do not display any company names or logos.

[Image 2: Finished Product]
The assembled three-dimensional papercraft. SD character, 2-3 heads tall. Hairstyle, hair color, clothing, and accessories are reproduced based on the input image. Paper texture, folds, rounded corners, and a handmade warmth. Composition and lighting suitable for product promotion photography.

[Caution]
Do not include any real company names, brand logos, or trademarks.

[Atmosphere]
Quality similar to photos in craft magazines or handmade works. Soft studio lighting, clean background. High resolution.
```

## Moutai Brand 3x3 Visual Storyboard

> Minimal, lüks logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A clean 3×3 storyboard grid with nine equal sized panels on 4:5 ratio.

Use the reference image as the base product reference. Keep the same product, packaging design, branding, materials, colors, proportions and overall identity across all nine panels exactly as the reference. The product must remain clearly recognizable in every frame. The label, logo and proportions must stay exactly the same.

This storyboard is a high-end designer mockup presentation for a branding portfolio. The focus is on form, composition, materiality and visual rhythm rather than realism or lifestyle narrative. The overall look should feel curated, editorial and design-driven.

**FRAME 1:**
Front-facing hero shot of the {argument name="product name" default="Moutai Baijiu"} in a clean studio setup. Neutral background, balanced composition, calm and confident presentation showing the complete product with bottle body relief and label hot stamping.

**FRAME 2:**
Close-up shot with the focus centered on the {argument name="product name" default="Moutai Baijiu"}'s label hot stamping. Focusing on glass and ceramic textures, precision craftsmanship, and liquid transparency.

**FRAME 3:**
Shows the {argument name="product name" default="Moutai Baijiu"} placed in a Chinese dining table environment. Studio setting inspired by high-end elegant aesthetics - clean lines, natural materials, soft lighting, creating an authentic brand atmosphere.

**FRAME 4:**
Product shown in use or interaction on a neutral studio background. Hands in natural gesture elegantly pouring the liquor, capturing the human-product connection, the look matches the high-end elegant aesthetic.

**FRAME 5:**
Isometric composition showing multiple {argument name="product name" default="Moutai Baijiu"} units arranged in a precise geometric order from the top isometric angle. All products placed at the same isometric top angle, evenly spaced, clean, structured and graphic.

**FRAME 6:**
The {argument name="product name" default="Moutai Baijiu"} levitating slightly tilted on a neutral background that matches the brand color palette. Floating position is angled and intentional, the product is floating naturally in space with dramatic lighting effects.

**FRAME 7:**
Extreme close-up focusing on specific detail of the {argument name="product name" default="Moutai Baijiu"} - bottle cap seal, material micro-texture, or precision engineering details.

**FRAME 8:**
The {argument name="product name" default="Moutai Baijiu"} in an unexpected yet aesthetically strong setting that feels bold, editorial and visually striking. Suspended in the moment of liquor splashing, studio-based and designer-driven, bold composition that elevates the brand.

**FRAME 9:**
Wide composition showing the product in use within a refined lifestyle setup. Clean props, controlled styling, cohesive with the rest of the series, suggesting the product's role in daily life.

**CAMERA & STYLE:**
Ultra high-quality studio imagery with a real camera look. Different camera angles and framings across frames. Controlled depth of field, precise lighting, accurate materials and reflections. dramatic contrast with golden highlights. Lighting logic, color palette, mood and visual language must remain consistent across all nine panels as one cohesive series.

**OUTPUT:**
A clean 3×3 grid with no borders, no text, no captions and no watermarks.
```

Ultra-realistic premium product photography of a sleek white aluminum soda can labeled “{argument name="soda flavor" default="DOC Soda Strawberry"},” floating diagonally across the frame at a dynamic tilted angle, surrounded by fresh whole strawberries and crystal-clear ice cubes suspended in mid-air. Vibrant translucent red strawberry soda liquid streams and splashes diagonally behind and around the can, forming elegant flowing ribbons with glossy highlights and scattered droplets frozen in motion. The strawberries appear ripe and saturated red with visible seeds and green leafy tops, while the ice cubes are highly transparent with sharp edges, internal reflections, and realistic refraction. The can surface is smooth matte aluminum with subtle metallic sheen, crisp typography, and clean graphic design. Shot in a controlled studio environment with a seamless soft red-to-pink gradient background and matching color palette, emphasizing freshness and flavor. Lighting is high-end commercial studio lighting with strong key light, soft fill, and rim highlights, producing sharp reflections on ice and liquid, soft shadows, and high contrast clarity.

Camera angle is slightly low and diagonal, medium close-up framing, shallow depth of field with razor-sharp focus on the can and foreground elements. Mood is energetic, refreshing, modern, and premium, hyper-photorealistic, cinematic advertising style, ultra-detailed, 8K, high-speed flash capture, luxury beverage brand.
```

## Image-to-Beverage Mockup Conversion

> Fotogerçekçi logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[AI Instruction: Image → Bottled Beverage Conversion]

Analyze [Input Image].

Extract: Read [Main Motif], [Atmosphere], and [Color Palette].
Drink Selection: Freely decide the drink based on the image's color and atmosphere (e.g., {argument name="Type of drink" default="fruit water, carbonated, tea, lactic acid beverage, energy drink, smoothie"}).
Label Design: Use the motif for the label illustration or logo. Colors should be reflected in the liquid color and the label.
Text: If there is text in the image, utilize it as the product name or flavor name.
[Main Motif]
A new product plastic bottle that catches the eye in the convenience store beverage aisle. The design should look as if the world view of [Input Image] has been transformed into a drink. [Detected Motif] decorates the label, and the liquid color matches the theme color. The bottle shape is also free (slim, round, can type, etc.).

[Atmosphere]
Product advertisement photo taken in a professional studio. The bottle has condensation, giving the texture of being cold. Fruits or ingredients may be scattered in the background. 8k, shallow depth of field at f/1.8, product photography lighting. Photorealistic.
```

## Luxury Korean Hanbok Product Mockup (Symmetrical Flat Lay)

> Minimal, lüks ürün maketi (mockup) üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A luxury Korean traditional Hanbok (한복) product mockup with perfectly balanced centered composition on seamless white background.

CANVAS & BACKGROUND - CRITICAL:
- Seamless infinity white backdrop fills entire frame edge to edge
- Background extends from corner to corner with no visible borders
- Clean warm white (#FFFAF5) continuous uniform tone across full canvas
- No vignetting, no falloff, no darkening at edges
- All four corners identical in color and brightness
- Even exposure across entire image from left to right, top to bottom
COMPOSITION - SYMMETRICAL CENTERED LAYOUT:
- Perfect center axis alignment
- Equal visual weight on left and right sides
- Products arranged in triangular hierarchy
- Minimum 10% margin between products and frame edges
BACK ROW (Top Center):
- CENTER-BACK: Matte black gift box with gold Dancheong border, 연꽃문 lotus embossed, "한스타일 Hstyle" in gold
- LEFT-BACK: Cream shopping bag with gold Taegeuk symbol
- RIGHT-BACK: Open box showing cream silk lining with gold crane pattern
MIDDLE ROW (Center Hero):
- EXACT CENTER: Crimson red silk Hanbok Jeogori (저고리) with gold peony embroidery on cream silk cloth, sleeves spread symmetrically, white collar (동정) visible
FRONT ROW (Evenly Spaced):
- LEFT: Traditional fan (부채) with plum blossom
- CENTER-LEFT: Plum blossom sprig
- CENTER: Royal blue Bokjumeoni (복주머니) with gold 福
- CENTER-RIGHT: Gold Binyeo (비녀) on cream cushion
- RIGHT: Plum blossom sprig
LIGHTING:
- Soft diffused overhead lighting
- Even illumination including all corners and edges
- No directional shadows toward frame edges
- Consistent brightness across full frame
CAMERA:
- Shot straight-on, camera perfectly centered
- 4:5 aspect ratio
- Products composed with safe margin from all edges
- Full frame background coverage
STYLE: Balanced editorial luxury, Korean heritage, commercial product photography
Negative prompt: vignetting, dark corners, uneven background, cropped edges, gradient falloff, visible backdrop edges, inconsistent lighting at borders
A stylish handwritten signature "Willy" in small gold letters at bottom right corner.
```

LAYOUT: Irregular grid (1+2) - large hero left, two supporting images stacked right

Background: Soft beige gradient, clean studio

SUPPORTING CELL 1 (top right, 20%):
Detail shot - watch on wrist, bias cut visible, hand touching watch
Background: Cream solid

SUPPORTING CELL 2 (bottom right, 20%):
Back view - watch detail, strap construction, elegant neckline
Background: Matching cream

PRODUCT INFO OVERLAY:
- Color swatches: 4 circles ({argument name="color 1" default="Silver"}, {argument name="color 2" default="Champagne"}, {argument name="color 3" default="Blush"}, {argument name="color 4" default="Gold"}) with names

TYPOGRAPHY:
"TIMELESS LUXURY" headline in elegant serif
"Elegance Redefined" tagline
Brand logo top left

CAMERA: Hasselblad X2D, 85mm for full body, 100mm macro for details, consistent soft studio lighting

Style keywords: collection showcase, irregular grid, chrome watch, minimal luxury, product presentation. A stylish handwritten signature Willy is elegantly and small letters placed at the Bottom Right corner.

## Luxury Product Photography for Artisanal Vanilla Coffee

> Sinematik, minimal ürün fotoğrafı üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Luxury product photography of artisanal vanilla ground coffee packaged in a soft sculpted ivory pouch with gentle wave contours, minimal branding embossed into fabric. The pouch rests on a rustic oak tabletop dusted with coffee grounds, accompanied by a hand-carved wooden scoop spilling aromatic powder.
Natural Madagascar vanilla pods curve organically around the base, blending into drifting coffee granules that form a subtle flowing aroma path instead of a spiral.
On the pouch front, a modern sensory chart: thin matte gold lines showing flavor balance — low acidity, rich sweetness, full-bodied depth.
Warm morning sunlight enters from the left at a low angle, creating soft shadows and tactile fabric highlights. Background fades into a cozy café interior with creamy bokeh lights, evoking calm morning rituals.
Ultra-sharp macro texture on vanilla pods and coffee grains, cinematic depth of field, premium lifestyle aesthetic, inviting, warm, handcrafted, high-end brand feel.
```

## Commercial Fashion Photograph with Strict Garment Preservation (Variant)

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "task_configuration": {
    "type": "GENERATE",
    "sub_type": "commercial_lifestyle_photograph",
    "reference_logic": "STRICT_GARMENT_PRESERVATION_FROM_INPUT",
    "aesthetic": "high_end_fashion_campaign",
    "output_quality": "high_resolution_photorealistic"
  },
  "subject_definition": {
    "identity": {
      "demographic": "white American female model",
      "physique": "fit",
      "appearance": "beautiful, naturally styled"
    },
    "skin_and_features": {
      "realism_rule": "macro_level_human_realism; NO artificial smoothing or filtering",
      "texture_details": [
        "visible natural pores",
        "light blemishes and imperfections",
        "soft expression lines around eyes/mouth",
        "light freckles across nose/cheeks",
        "natural skin highlights and subsurface scattering"
      ],
      "makeup": "minimal, natural or bare skin look"
    },
    "attire_constraints": {
      "source": "garment_from_input_reference_image",
      "preservation_rules": [
        "exact color dye and pattern",
        "exact fabric weave and texture",
        "exact thread stitching details",
        "exact fit, drape, and silhouette on the body"
      ],
      "modification_permission": "NONE"
    },

## Solarpunk Product Showcase with Bioluminescence

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A stunning solarpunk product showcase featuring an elegant grid arrangement of sustainable technology products seamlessly integrated with lush botanical elements. The scene displays bio-engineered devices with flowing organic curves and living moss accents arranged in perfect symmetry on a reclaimed wood surface. Each product emanates a soft bioluminescent glow, featuring translucent bio-plastic casings with visible internal plant root systems and flowing water channels. The background reveals a verdant living wall cascade with hanging air-purifying vines, integrated solar panel leaves, and delicate flowering pods that power the displayed technology.

Golden hour sunlight streams through floor-to-ceiling windows, casting warm amber rays that dance across the green foliage and illuminate the products with a dreamy, ethereal backlight. Soft volumetric lighting creates gentle lens flares and highlights the organic textures of both nature and technology. The atmosphere feels optimistic and serene, embodying a harmonious future where innovation and nature coexist beautifully.

Shot from a slightly elevated three-quarter angle using a wide-angle lens with shallow depth of field, creating artistic bokeh in the background vegetation while keeping the product grid in sharp focus. The composition follows rule of thirds with leading lines created by the wooden planks and vine arrangements. Professional commercial photography style with vibrant color grading emphasizing rich emerald greens, warm golden tones, and creamy white highlights. The aesthetic balances minimalist {argument name="design style" default="Korean"} design sensibilities with abundant natural elements, creating an Instagram-worthy visual that suggests premium eco-luxury and sustainable innovation. Photorealistic rendering with high dynamic range, crisp details, and subtle film grain for organic authenticity.
A stylish handwritten signature Willy is elegantly and small letters placed at the Bottom Right corner.
```

## Character Ekiben (Lunch Box) Product Shot

> Fotogerçekçi ürün fotoğrafı üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Character Name: {argument name="キャラクター名" default="[Character Name]"}
(Scene/Moment)
A single shot capturing the moment the lid of a character-themed lunch box (Ekiben style), packaged for sale, is opened.

The transparent or paper lid is partially covering the lunch box, revealing the contents.

(Packaging/Lid Expression)
The outside of the lid is printed with a commercial package design based on the character in the reference image. Various exaggerated sales phrases based on the reference image are displayed with high quality and high sense.

Design that looks like it would be lined up in an actual Ekiben stall.
Illustration of the character deformed with a 'food' theme.
Limited color palette, layout resembling food labels, logos, and catchphrases.
Display a price that greatly reflects the concept of the reference image and the lunch box.

Includes a humorous product name that utilizes the character's personality.
If a character name is set at the beginning, use it.
Realistic depiction of print gloss, paper thickness, and folded parts.
*This lid is strongly linked to the concept of the contents.
(Composition/Shooting Style)
Shoot the lunch box placed on a table from a **slightly diagonal top-down perspective (product photography flat lay)**.

8K resolution, ultra-realistic food photography.

Quality that would not look out of place in a convenience store, department store basement, or Ekiben fair.
(Container - Lunch Box)
A mass-produced, realistic lunch box container.

Common lunch box containers such as black, white, or wood grain.
With dividers or integrated.
Realistic depiction of rounded corners, molding marks, and shadows on the rim.

Basic Structure of Character Representation
Face/Body:

Three-dimensionally shaped rice (white rice/seasoned rice/mixed rice, etc., changing according to the concept).
Expression/Outline:

Three-dimensionally expressed using nori (seaweed), salted kelp, sesame seeds, fried pasta, chopped vegetables, etc.
Concept-Specific Side Dishes
The concept exaggerates the characteristics of the reference image.
Not a fixed menu, but changes freely according to the character and world view.
All side dishes are composed only of real ingredients and cooking methods,

Each one has a solid three-dimensional feel and texture.
(Color Design)
Do not use the colors of the reference image directly,
Translate using only natural colors as food ingredients.
White/light brown of rice.
Golden brown of fried food.
Green of vegetables.
Red of tomato/plum/carrot.
Yellow of egg.
Black of nori/sesame.
Designed to recognize the character not by 'color coding' but by
Differences in texture, shape, and arrangement.
(Text/Logo Expression: Reinforcing the feeling of a product for sale)
Three-dimensional expression of the product name and catchphrase using food items in the empty space within the lunch box.

Exaggerate the characteristics of the reference image.
The text is composed entirely of “edible items.”
(Background/Environment)
Randomly select an outdoor setting.
Natural light from the side.
Emphasize the shine of oil on fried chicken, the moisture of the rice, and the freshness of the vegetables.
The overall color temperature is warm, with light that makes it look the most delicious at the point of sale.
(Overall Tone)
“Cute” + “Looks like it could actually be sold.”
Fusion of Character Bento × Product Photography × Food Sizzle.
```

## Flat Lay Fashion Editorial Image

> Minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[OUTFIT_STYLE] = Use the outfit from the reference image | casual utilitarian womenswear | casual mens streetwear
[COMPOSITION_SCHEME] = seated-inspired angular layout | standing-inspired vertical layout | walking-inspired offset layout | dynamic diagonal layout | balance-inspired asymmetric layout | editorial flat-lay grid break | sculptural negative-space layout | yoga-inspired angular balance layout | athletic-inspired directional layout
[SURFACE] = clean matte studio backdrop | soft neutral paper surface | cool tone matte backdrop
[ACCESSORY_MODE] = outfit_integrated | decorative | none

Make a high resolution flat lay fashion image for editorial and catalog use.
Use a strict top down camera angle with a natural 50mm equivalent perspective and perfectly parallel framing.
Set the scene on [SURFACE] with no visible texture seams or edge shadows.

Show only clothing items and approved accessories placed on the surface.
Do not depict or imply any human body, body parts, limbs, skin, joints, or anatomy.
Do not include legs, hips, feet, knees, or any biological structure.

Arrange garments as a flat, uninhabited composition following [COMPOSITION_SCHEME].
Interpret [COMPOSITION_SCHEME] purely as a geometric layout logic, never as a body configuration.
Use angles, spacing, and directional alignment only to suggest balance and posture.

Keep every garment fully flat with zero internal volume.
Force all bottom garments including pants or leggings to remain collapsed, empty, and tension-free.
Do not stretch, contour, wrap, or compress fabric.
Represent bends only through sharp planar folds and angular breaks.
Prohibit smooth curves, rounded forms, seated volume, or body-fit deformation.

Allow folds and wrinkles only as gravity-driven surface creases.
Ensure clothing reads strictly as laid fabric, not worn, inhabited, or shaped by a body.

Select garments and accessories based on {argument name="outfit style" default="OUTFIT_STYLE"}.
If [ACCESSORY_MODE] is outfit_integrated, include only functionally worn items.
If [ACCESSORY_MODE] is decorative, allow accessories as independent flat objects.
If [ACCESSORY_MODE] is none, exclude all accessories entirely.

Use soft diffused daylight from above with gentle shadows directly beneath each item.
Keep spacing balanced with visible negative space around the composition.
Avoid mannequins, models, body silhouettes, stuffing, air filled shapes, extra items, text, logos, or branding.
Ensure clarity, accurate fabric texture, and a calm contemporary aesthetic.
```

## Fashion Catalog Item Extraction from Reference Image

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal ürün fotoğrafı üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

You are a professional fashion catalog creator.
Analyze the clothing worn by the man in the reference image.
Based strictly on the actual designs of the clothes he is wearing, extract and separate them into the following four categories:

① Outerwear / jacket / coat
② Shirt / innerwear / dress shirt
③ Pants / slacks / denim
④ Shoes / sneakers / leather shoes

Each extracted item must accurately reflect the original clothing’s color, material texture, shape, and details.
Do NOT redesign, modify, stylize, or reinterpret the clothing in any way.
Do NOT show the human body. Extract and display the clothing items only.
The output image must be:
・white background
・front-facing view
・no shadows, no noise
・high-quality product photography, apparel e-commerce style
Arrange the four items neatly in either a horizontal row or a 2×2 grid.
Add Japanese labels to each item exactly as follows:

① Outerwear / jacket / coat, etc.
② Shirt / innerwear / dress shirt, etc.
③ Pants / slacks / denim, etc.
④ Shoes / sneakers / leather shoes, etc.

Create a clean, easy-to-read catalog-style image.
ultra high resolution, photorealistic, studio product lighting, flat lighting, extremely detailed fabric texture, apparel catalog quality

Negative prompt:
anime, illustration, cartoon, stylized, redesigned clothing, fashion sketch, mannequin, human body, model wearing clothes, background, shadow, reflection, blur, low quality, noisy image, dramatic lighting
```

– Authentic [{argument name="country cuisine" default="COUNTRY"}] cuisine presentation

– Natural gravity realism, frozen motion
– No mess, clean and controlled composition

Lighting & camera:
– Controlled studio lighting emphasizing texture and shine
– High-speed photography look
– Shallow to medium depth of field

– Luxury food advertising aesthetic
– Rich contrast, warm natural tones
– Ultra-sharp focus, no artificial glow

Negative prompt:
Text, logos, branding, hands, people, utensils, plates, tables, cartoon style, burnt food, overexposure, low-detail textures, artificial shine.

The bottle rests inside an organic pool of soft pink foamy bubbles, with airy soap lather spreading outward in smooth, rounded shapes. Small transparent bubbles float around the foam surface. Clean matte pastel pink background with subtle gradient. Soft diffused top lighting with gentle rim light creates a luminous glow through the liquid, highlighting the pump tube inside the bottle. Sharp focus, high clarity, realistic liquid refraction, smooth plastic and glass textures, luxury beauty advertising style, modern cosmetic branding, ultra-realistic, 8K detail, high dynamic range.

Negative Prompt:

hands, people, faces, clutter, harsh reflections, label distortion, unreadable text, warped pump, hard shadows, grain, noise, low resolution, CGI look, cartoon style, messy foam, uneven lighting, watermark
```

## Futuristic Sci-Fi Still Life Photography Template

```
Cinematic sci-fi still life photography of {argument name="object name" default="[Item Name]"} in {argument name="scene type" default="[Scene Type]"}. The [Item] is constructed from frosted borosilicate glass with dichroic polished aluminum accents. Translucent resin [Component]. Bioluminescent ice blue glow accents. The [Item] [Dynamic Presentation Method] [Scene Element]. Extremely faint glowing energy field particles [Particle Effect]. Minimal composition with only the [Item] and [Scene Element]. Dramatic side rim lighting emphasizing the [Dynamic Effect] and translucency. The glowing parts act as auxiliary light sources. Deep, clean cold-toned gradient background from deep space gray to nebula blue. Ultra-clean, organized, minimalist composition.
Scene Type Options
A. Anti-Gravity:

The [Item] floats several centimeters above a sleek glowing magnetic field generator. Extremely faint glowing energy field particles surround the floating object in a perfectly symmetrical pattern.
B. Interactive Surface:

The [Item] sits on a perfectly clean holographic [Surface Type] surface with minimal data visualization - only a few simple glowing lines showing [Data Effect].
C. Structural Suspension:

The [Item] is suspended between several perfectly aligned, ultra-thin glowing tension cables in a symmetrical [Arrangement] arrangement.
Dynamic Presentation Methods
Anti-Gravity → Floating, Drifting, Suspended
Interactive Surface → Activated, Generated, Displayed
Structural Suspension → Suspended, Captured, Supported
Item Description Section
TEXT OVERLAYS: 
Bottom Left: \"{argument name="object english name" default="[Item English Name]"} / Frosted Glass & Aluminum\" in Helvetica Neue Light, color #E0E0E0.
Bottom Right: \"{argument name="brand name" default="[Brand Name]"}\" brand name in bold modern sans-serif font with a sophisticated logo featuring an abstract geometric symbol combining [Element 1] and [Element 2] design with [Detail Description], both in color #E0E0E0.
```

## Realistic Interior to Transparent Miniature Diorama Prompt

> Lüks mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Based on the uploaded "realistic interior space" reference image, generate a "transparent miniature house display model."

[Core Display Concept]
- Transform the original realistic interior space into a "miniature-scale transparent architectural model"
- Like an architectural cross-section model displayed in a museum
- The house is completely enclosed in a transparent acrylic / glass shell
- The interior structure and furniture are fully visible

[Structure and Layout (Must be Strictly Retained)]
- Strictly inherit the interior layout and proportions from the reference image
- Wall positions, furniture placement, and circulation paths must be identical
- The room is scaled down entirely to a miniature proportion (e.g., 1:10 / 1:12)
- This is proportional scaling, not redesign

[Transparent Casing Design]
- Exterior is high-transparency acrylic or glass material
- Rounded corners, clearly visible thickness
- Local use of cutaway / angled structures for easy viewing of the interior
- The transparent casing does not obstruct the main interior view

[Interior Representation (Miniature but Realistic)]
- All furniture is scaled down proportionally
- Materials remain realistic: wood grain, fabric, metal
- Retain a sense of real life, not a toy feel
- Lighting fixtures, plants, and ornaments are clearly visible

[Lighting System (Key Focus)]
- Warm-toned micro-lights inside (like model lights)
- Neutral or cool-toned display lights outside
- Creates a high-end display contrast of warm interior and cool exterior
- Gently glowing but not dazzling

[Display Environment]
- Placed on a minimalist pedestal / museum display stand
- Clean background (light gray / dark gray / black)
- Includes a real scale reference (like the stand's shadow)

[Visual Style]
- Hyper-realistic
- High-end industrial design aesthetic
- Looks like a display piece from Apple / Muji / an architectural firm
- Not toy-like, not cartoonish

[Photography and Composition]
- Professional product photography or architectural model photography
- Medium-close shot composition, slightly overhead view
- Shallow depth of field, blurred background
- Clear focus on the transparent miniature house itself

[Technical Requirements]
- Ultra-high definition details
- High refractive index, realistic glass representation
- Correct reflections and perspective
- No people, no text, no watermarks

Displayed as an art installation, possessing contemporary design and collectible value
Healing, exquisite, visually striking effect that immediately captures attention
Avoid toy texture, avoid plastic feel, emphasize architectural model and industrial design texture
High transparency, clear refractive edges, clean glass
Interior lighting clearly visible, like a miniature model lighting system
```

Start on a soft pastel pink background, empty and still. A silver aluminum can labeled “life by colis – Sparkling Strawberry – Certified Organic” slowly fades into view, standing slightly tilted, covered in realistic condensation droplets. Ambient studio lighting is soft and premium.

Suddenly, a fresh strawberry drops from above in slow motion and strikes the top of the can. End on a hero frame matching a luxury beverage advertisement dynamic yet elegant, hyper-realistic, high contrast, crisp details, no camera shake, no extra elements added.

## High-End Concept Art Magazine Editorial Photograph Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[BRAND NAME]: 
A high-end, glossy concept art magazine editorial photograph of a unique, unexpected functional object conceptualized and designed by the brand.

**1. The Concept & Object (AI Invention):**
Based on the design philosophy, heritage, and material vocabulary of the specified brand, the AI must invent a novel utility product (NOT standard clothing, shoes, or bags). Examples could be home goods, tech accessories, tools, or sporting equipment, reinterpretated through the brand's lens. The object should feel sculptural yet functional.

**2. Materials & Details (Hyper-Premium):**
The object is constructed from ultra-premium, highly tactile materials characteristic of the brand (e.g., patinated exotic leathers, brushed aerospace-grade titanium, sculpted matte ceramics, molded carbon fiber, or technical high-fashion textiles). Every detail is hyper-realistic: visible stitching, microscopic material grain, precision engravings, and complex texture contrasts.

**3. Photography & Lighting (Cinematic Studio):**
Shot on a medium format Phase One camera with a 100mm macro lens. Extremely shallow depth of field, with sharp focus on the hero details of the object and a creamy, smooth bokeh background. The lighting is sophisticated studio softbox lighting: gentle, enveloping fill light with precise rim lighting to accentuate contours and material textures.

**4. Environment:**
A seamless, impeccably clean studio cyclorama background in a pure, ultra-light pastel tone (e.g., desaturated mint, pale blush, or off-white), free of shadows.

**5. Layout & UI Elements (Strict Placement):**
- **Bottom Right Corner:** A small, understated, monochrome gray logo of the brand.
- **Bottom Left Corner:** Small, minimalist monochrome gray text describing the invented product. The font style looks like Manrope Regular with very tight tracking (kerning) and balanced line spacing. Example format: "CONCEPT STUDY: [AI inserts invented product name]. MATERIAL: [AI inserts main materials]. SS25."
```

## Pixel Art Grid using Fruit Flat-Lay Photography Prompt

> Oyun görseli üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
# Scene and World
Professional top-down flat-lay photography, strictly 90-degree vertical overhead God's perspective;
Pure white background;
Studio soft lighting, uniform and clear light, no shadows, no vignetting

# Focus and Order
Absolute Subject: A grid matrix formed by tightly and neatly arranged [{argument name="background item" default="fresh yellow lemons"}] (the background item), filling the entire frame;
Grid Specification: {argument name="grid columns" default="27"} columns horizontally, and several rows vertically, forming a complete matrix;
Form of Expression: Using [{argument name="contrast item" default="green limes"}] (the contrast item) with stark color contrast embedded to form a clear pixel art pattern;
Visual Logic: The upper half of the frame is the pixelated "{argument name="text content" default="2026"}" formed by the contrast item, and the lower half is the pixelated silhouette of the {argument name="graphic content" default="running horse"} formed by the contrast item;
Arrangement Constraint: All objects are exactly the same size, uniformly oriented (stems pointing the same way), tightly and seamlessly fitted together, absolutely no blank areas allowed

# Form and Structure
Upper composition: Pixelated "{text content}" using the contrast item, employing a monospace font where each character occupies the same number of columns;
Lower composition: Pixelated silhouette of the {graphic content} using the contrast item;
Perfect overhead 90-degree flat-lay perspective, camera lens axis strictly perpendicular to the ground;
Every object is whole, objects at the edge of the frame are not cropped, presenting a neat grid array;
Pixel art pattern is clearly discernible, edges are sharp;
Dense grid layout of {grid columns} columns × multiple rows, objects tightly filled, no gaps whatsoever

# Material and Language
Main Material: Real [{background item}] (smooth surface, natural reflection, fine texture, all objects uniformly oriented);
Contrast Material: Real [{contrast item}] (full color, fine surface texture, real details, all objects uniformly oriented);
Background: Pure white plane;
All objects are uniform in size, shape, and orientation, with no size differences or deformation

# Source of Tension
Natural objects transformed into digital pixel art;
Combination of traditional still life photography and modern pixel patterns;
Strong color contrast between [{background item}] and [{contrast item}] creates clear pattern recognition;
8K quality, high resolution, commercial advertising photography style;
Objects are absolutely regular in arrangement, pixel pattern is highly readable

# Technical Constraints (Ensuring Stability)
- Strictly 90-degree overhead angle, no perspective distortion
- Objects at the edge of the frame must be complete, not cropped
- The diameter of every object must be exactly the same, as if made from the same mold
- All objects must be uniformly oriented, stems or tops pointing consistently
- Clear grid structure: {grid columns} columns horizontally, and corresponding rows vertically, forming a strict rectangular grid
- Grid arrangement must be neat, aligned horizontally and vertically, with each cell the same size
- Objects must be tightly and seamlessly joined, absolutely no blank areas or gaps allowed
- Edges of the pixel pattern must be sharp and crisp
- Light is uniform, no local overexposure or underexposure
- Each character uses a monospace pixel font, all characters occupy the same number of columns
- Each object acts as one pixel unit, must be exactly the same size and uniformly oriented
```

## 3D Hagoita (Japanese Battledore) Image Generation Prompt

> 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{argument name="Character features from reference image" default="The woman in the attached image. Regenerate her to look sexy."}: The woman in the attached image. Regenerate her to look sexy.

{argument name="Attire" default="The luxurious kimono in the image"}: The luxurious kimono in the image

{argument name="Sign text" default="lion"}: lion

A high-quality photograph of a traditional Japanese New Year's decoration, a 'Hagoita' (battledore), displayed inside a glass case with a luxurious white wood frame.
The image is a vertical portrait size suitable for a smartphone screen.

Angle and Composition:
The Hagoita is captured from a 'diagonal front' angle. By applying an angle instead of a straight front view, the thickness of the Hagoita and the three-dimensional relief of the 'Oshie' (appliqué) are emphasized, creating a sense of depth.

Hagoita Design:
In the center of the Hagoita, a three-dimensional character created using the traditional Japanese craft technique 'Oshie' is placed.

Character Features: {argument name="Character features from reference image" default="The woman in the attached image. Regenerate her to look sexy."} The character is not full-body; only the 'upper half' is largely sculpted. The feet and hem are not depicted.

Character Attire: Made from vibrant, textured Japanese fabrics (Chirimen or Kinran) in the style of {argument name="Attire" default="The luxurious kimono in the image"}.

The sleeves and decorations of the kimono have a dynamic design, significantly extending beyond the physical edges of the board to the left and right, spreading into the air. The fabric is layered, creating an overwhelming sense of volume and three-dimensionality.

Background Board Surface: The surface of the board behind the character is not plain wood but is lavishly decorated with 'gold leaf' or 'gold-painted designs of cherry blossoms and fans,' making it gorgeously ornate. Traditional Japanese patterns cover every space, making it extremely brilliant.

Handle (Grip): The handle part is not just a wooden stick but is decorated with 'black lacquer' with gold patterns, or wrapped with beautiful 'decorative cords' and 'tassels.'

Accessories:
A decorative 'Temari' (handball) is placed at the base of the Hagoita.
A white wood stand (tate-kanban) is placed next to (in front of) the Hagoita. The stand also has thickness, and the wood texture is realistic.

[IMPORTANT]: The text on the stand is artistically written in a 'masterful, flowing brush script (Gyosho style)' as if written by a skilled craftsman, spelling out "{argument name="Sign text" default="lion"}". The style conveys the blurring of the ink and the force of the brush.

Background and Atmosphere:
The background of the glass case features light pink and gold Japanese patterns (cherry blossoms or flowing water motifs).
There is light reflection on the glass case, and high-quality, luxurious lighting illuminates the piece.

Photorealistic style, 8k resolution, extremely detailed.
```

## Luxury Fukubukuro (Lucky Bag) Advertisement Key Visual Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, lüks afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Character Name: {argument name="Character Name" default="[Your Character Name]"}

## Key Visual Generation Prompt for Advertising/Posters/Promotional Materials (Lucky Bag Edition)
### 0. Primary Goal of This Prompt (Highest Priority)
This prompt is intended to generate **"photorealistic images of lucky bags" and key visuals for advertisements, posters, and promotional materials for department stores, specialty shops, and limited sales.**
The generated image must satisfy the following priorities:
* A level of completion that is instantly recognizable as an "advertisement."
* The lucky bag must function as the "main product," while the surrounding contents, world view, and character elements intensely boost anticipation and narrative.
### 1. Overall Premise of the Visual (Advertising Declaration | Boost Design)
This key visual is based on an advertising design where the direction of expression varies greatly, starting from the characteristics of the reference image. Do not aim for a stable solution; instead, extract the strongest element among luxury, excitement, strangeness, humor, chaos, or specialness, and elevate it into a **high-quality, high-sense, and "genius expression that makes you want it even though all the contents are visible."** It is an absolute condition that it functions as an "advertisement valid at a department store's first sale." This boost result is consistently reflected in the composition, placement of contents, product name display, and copy.
### 2. Scene/Moment (The Moment Cut Out for Advertising)
Automatically select the moment that has the highest appeal as an advertisement and instantly conveys "what kind of experience the lucky bag contains."
* The lucky bag may be opened.
* It may be half-open with contents spilling out.
* The contents may be beautifully arranged around the lucky bag.
**In this prompt, the composition with "the lucky bag at the center, with contents spread around it" is prioritized.**
Cut out a state where, upon first glance,
"What's inside?"
"But it's not ordinary."
"I want this."
all arise simultaneously.

### 3. Composition/Shooting Style (Advertising Layout Premise)

* Place the lucky bag in the center to slightly foreground of the screen.
* Frontal to slightly overhead angle suitable for advertising.
* Arrange the contents around the lucky bag in a circular, radial, or floating manner.
* Place product name text near or overlapping each item.

* Ensure intentional negative space for placing copy and year.
**Technical Requirements:**
* 8K resolution
* Ultra-realistic product photo
* Department store advertisement quality
### 4. Lucky Bag Package Design
The lucky bag is designed as the "outer shell" that most purely reflects the characteristics of the reference image.
* Basically, a shape that is valid as a department store, long-established shop, or brand lucky bag.
* Material (paper, cloth, Japanese paper, vinyl, luxury textile, etc.) is free.
* Change freely between luxury/pop/strange/festive/foreign object feeling, depending on the impression of the reference image.

### 5. Contents (Product Persuasiveness)
The contents of the lucky bag are not fixed by genre and are reconstructed based on the reference image and concept.
* Products that seem real.
* Products that are impossible.
* Characterized products.
* Products with strange sizes.
may be mixed.
The important thing is to establish the feeling that **"I can see everything, but it's mysterious and I want it."**

### 6. Character Expression (Inhabitants of the Lucky Bag World)
The character exists naturally within the contents of the lucky bag or the surrounding world.
* May be composed of products.
* May be jumping out of the lucky bag.
* May be in a pose introducing the contents.

Exclude any artificial feeling,

Treat them as "beings who originally live in this lucky bag world."
### 7. Color Scheme
The color rules are not fixed; the optimal solution is automatically selected based on the reference image and concept.
* Express value not through color, but through texture, density, gloss, and information volume.
* The lucky bag, products, characters, and background should not clash, yet be excessively lively.
### 8. Advertising Copy/Product Name Display (Most Important)
Copy and product names naturally arise as a result of verbalizing the reference image and concept.

* Directly display "Product Name Text" next to each item placed around the lucky bag.
* Product names allow for naming that is high-sense/intensely humorous/barely nonsensical.
* Year
* Lucky Bag Title
* Words like Limited/First Sale/Carefully Crafted
* Price notation
Are placed in the most beautiful and most insane arrangement for an advertisement.
Treat text as a **visual element, not information.**
### 9. Variation Display (For Advertising)
* Display lucky bag variations like Pine/Bamboo/Plum
* Normal/Deluxe/Insane
In a wipe-style display.
* Small frame
* Lucky Bag Name + Price
* Size that does not obstruct the main subject
---
### 10. Background/Light
* Bright, hopeful light of the first sale.
* Changes to gorgeous/pop/strange/serene, depending on the reference image.
* Maximize the texture of materials, logos, and the edges of text.
---
### 11. Final Tone (Output Guideline)
**Looks genuinely for sale,
all contents are visible,
yet clearly not ordinary.**
Character x Lucky Bag x Advertising Key Visual
Completed form.
```

## Premium Winter Product Photography in Alpine Setting

> Lüks logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A single, high-quality product item positioned centrally in the snow.
(Note for generation:

Insert specific object here, e.g., "{argument name="product example 1" default="A white ceramic teapot with gold trim and 'WINTERBREW' logo"}," "{argument name="product example 2" default="A clear glass perfume bottle with silver cap and 'ALPINE ESSENCE' text"}," or "{argument name="product example 3" default="A pair of brown leather hiking boots covered in light snow"}.")
accessories
Natural, thematic elements scattered artfully on the immediate snow surface surrounding the main subject. Examples include pinecones, whole cinnamon sticks, star anise, frosted lavender sprigs, small pine branches, or complementary items like a knit beanie.
environment
A pristine, sunlit winter alpine landscape. The immediate foreground is deep, textured powder snow. The mid-ground features snow-laden evergreen trees. The background is dominated by majestic, rugged alpine mountain peaks under a clear or slightly hazy blue sky. A rustic wooden chalet or cabin is visible in the middle distance, slightly blurred.
lighting
Bright, crisp, natural daylight. Sunny conditions creating sparkling highlights on the snow crystals and the product surface, casting distinct, cool shadows.
camera
Professional commercial photography. High resolution, macro detail sharp focus on the main subject. Shallow depth of field (bokeh), creating a softly blurred background of the mountains and trees. Shot from a slightly low or eye-level angle relative to the product placed on the snow.
style
High-end product photography with a cozy, luxurious, fresh, and serene winter atmosphere. The composition is balanced and naturalistic.
```

## 3D Hagoita (Battledore) Prompt for Nano Banana Pro

> Fotogerçekçi 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
# Information Input: Please rewrite the contents within [ ].

[Character Features]: {argument name="Character Features" default="Anime-style girl. Brown medium hair, red eyes, slightly pointed teeth (gapped teeth)."}
[Attire]: {argument name="Attire" default="Luxurious Furisode Kimono"} (*Enter "Luxurious Furisode Kimono" for women, "Montsuki Hakama" for men, etc.)
[Signboard Text]: {argument name="Signboard Text" default="Tsuno-chan"}

High-quality photograph of a "Hagoita" (traditional Japanese New Year's decoration) displayed inside a glass case with a luxurious white wood frame.
The image is a vertical portrait size suitable for a smartphone screen.

Angle and Composition:
An angle capturing the Hagoita from "diagonally forward." By adding an angle instead of a frontal view, the thickness of the Hagoita and the three-dimensional relief of the Oshie (appliqué) are emphasized, creating a sense of depth.

Hagoita Design:
In the center of the Hagoita, a three-dimensional character created using the traditional Japanese craft technique "Oshie" is placed.
Character Features: [Character Features] The character is not full-body, but only the "upper body" is largely sculpted. Feet and hem are not drawn.
Character Attire: [Attire] made from vibrant, textured Japanese fabrics (Chirimen or Kinran).
The sleeves and decorations of the kimono are dynamic, extending widely to the left and right beyond the physical edges of the board, spreading into the air.
The fabric is layered multiple times, creating an overwhelming sense of volume and three-dimensionality.
Background Board Surface: The surface of the board behind the character is not just plain wood, but gorgeously decorated with "gold leaf" or "gold paintings of cherry blossoms and fans." Traditional Japanese patterns are drawn without gaps, making it extremely brilliant.
Handle (Grip): The handle part is not just a wooden stick, but decorated with "black lacquer" and gold patterns, or wrapped with beautiful "decorative cords" and "tassels."

Accessories:
A decorative "Temari" (handball) is placed at the base of the Hagoita.
A white wooden sign (standing signboard) is placed next to (in front of) the Hagoita. The sign also has thickness, and the wood texture is realistic.
[Important]: The text on the sign is written artistically in a "masterful, flowing brush script (Gyosho style)" as if written by a skilled craftsman, stating "[Signboard Text]." The style conveys the blurring and momentum of the ink brush.

Background and Atmosphere:
The background of the glass case features faint pink or gold Japanese patterns (cherry blossoms or flowing water patterns).
There is light reflection on the glass case, and high-quality lighting is illuminating it.
Photorealistic style, 8k resolution, extremely detailed.
```
