## Visual Creator Mode Prompt for Product Photography (Precision vs. Decay)

> Ürün fotoğrafı üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
## 🎨 Visual Creator Mode

You are a top visual creative director with AI image generation capabilities.

I will provide a 'Creative Skeleton' randomly generated by a program, in JSON format. Your task is:

1.  **Interpret and Optimize**: Understand the various dimensions in the JSON (theme, tension, light/shadow, scene, etc.) and integrate them into a coherent, visually impactful concept.
2.  **First, display the optimized prompt**: Output the final image generation prompt you will use (in English, approximately 50-100 words) in a code block (```prompt ... ```).
3.  **Then, directly generate the image**: Use the above prompt to generate a high-quality visual work.

---

### Follow these principles during optimization:

-   **Resolve Conflicts**: If the dimension combination has logical contradictions (e.g., 'Industrial Photography' with 'Miniature Fantasy'), choose a dominant style and harmonize.
-   **Visual Anchor**: Translate abstract tension (e.g., 'Whimsical vs. Real Material') into concrete visual elements (e.g., melted chocolate flowing over precise metal gears).
-   **Viral Hook**: The image must have an unexpected detail that 'makes people want to look twice.'
-   **Style Tone**: If a `creative_directive` exists, use it as the emotional guidance for the image; if an `oblique_strategy` exists, use it as a structural constraint.

---

### Program Generated Creative Skeleton (JSON Input):
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "creative_id": "v9jrcpus",
  "creative_world": "world:product_photography",
  "creation_intent": "Form or Material Exploration",
  "creation_intent_id": "explore",
  "generation_logic": "The process or making itself is the highlight",
  "generation_logic_id": "process-driven",
  "twist_mechanisms": [
    "Scale Mismatch",
    "Cutaway Logic",
    "Material Swap"
  ],
  "twist_ids": [
    "scale_mismatch",
    "cutaway_logic",
    "material_swap"
  ],
  "subject_kit": {
    "primary_subject": "{argument name="primary subject" default="Mechanical Keyboard Keycaps"}",
    "primary_id": "a-mechanical-keyboard-keycap-set",
    "secondary_elements": [
      "Dust",
      "Tiny Gears",
      "Spec Label"
    ],
    "secondary_ids": [
      "element:dust",
      "element:tiny-gears",
      "element:spec-label"
    ]
  },
  "core_tension": "Precision vs Decay",
  "core_tension_id": "precision_vs_decay",
  "stage_context": "White Cyclorama",
  "stage_context_id": "white-cyclorama",
  "composition_rule": "Two-Object Comparison Layout",
  "composition_rule_id": "two-object-comparison-layout",
  "lighting_rule": "Three-Point Professional Lighting",
  "lighting_rule_id": "three-point-professional-lighting",
  "imaging_assumption": "Industrial Product Photography",
  "imaging_assumption_id": "industrial_product_photography",
  "deliverable_type": "Product Photo",
  "emergence": {
    "score": 2,
    "label": "Interesting Combo / Fun Combination"
  },
  "final_prompt": "Product Photo. featuring Mechanical Keyboard Keycaps, Dust, Tiny Gears, Spec Label. inspired by Precision vs Decay. with Scale Mismatch, Cutaway Logic, Material Swap. set in White Cyclorama. following Two-Object Comparison Layout. lit by Three-Point Professional Lighting.",
  "creative_directive": "Treat failure as the final work",
  "oblique_strategy": {
    "id": "reverse_assumption",
    "desc": "Reverse a fundamental assumption"
  },
  "validation": {
    "errors": [],
    "warnings": [],
    "dropped_overrides": []
  }
}
```

## Multi-Language Figure Product Introduction Layout Prompt for Nano Banana Pro

> Logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[Figure Product Introduction Style Layout Prompt – Multi-Language Compatible Version]

This prompt allows you to transform a character from a reference image into a collectible figure
and present it in a product introduction–style layout.
The display language can be selected from four options:
Japanese, English, German, or French.

Please read this description carefully until the end.

※ This prompt is intended exclusively for Nano Banana Pro.
※ Unauthorized reproduction or redistribution of this prompt is prohibited.

◆ Overview

Display Language (language)
You can choose from:
- "ja" (Japanese)
- "en" (English)
- "de" (German)
- "fr" (French)

Product Name (product_name)
Manufacturer Name (manufacturer_name)

You may specify any arbitrary strings for both values.

Product Name Logo:
The product name logo is inferred from the product name string
and the visual atmosphere of the reference image.

Manufacturer Logo:
The manufacturer logo is inferred from the manufacturer name string.

※ Depending on the selected display language, these names may be translated
   or localized automatically.

Size:
The total height (in centimeters) is inferred and displayed
based on the character’s body proportions and the 1/7 scale setting.

Material:
Appropriate materials for a collectible figure are inferred and displayed.

Price:
The retail price is inferred from the figure’s scale,
modeling density, and paint detailing.

Close-Up Views:
Each close-up view is accompanied by a caption
that explains a different part or feature of the figure.
All captions are generated in the selected display language.

◆ How to Use

Display Language (language)
Rewrite the content inside the quotation marks ("") as shown below:

language: "{argument name="display language" default="en"}"

Available values:
ja: Japanese
en: English
de: German
fr: French

Product Name (product_name)
Manufacturer Name (manufacturer_name)

Rewrite the content inside the quotation marks ("")
using any arbitrary strings you like.

The manufacturer name will also be reflected
in the "Sculptor / Production" field.

product_name: "{argument name="product name" default="ARBITRARY_PRODUCT_NAME"}"
manufacturer_name: "{argument name="manufacturer name" default="ARBITRARY_MANUFACTURER_NAME"}"

◆ Precautions for Using This Prompt

This prompt does not intentionally include any specific work titles,
author names, character names, or brand names.

However, due to the internal processing or training data
of the image generation model, unintended associations may occur.
As a result, generated images may resemble existing works,
characters, trademarks, or brand logos.

Please exercise sufficient caution when using, publishing,
or distributing generated images.

We assume no responsibility whatsoever for any outcomes produced
by using this prompt, or for any issues, damages, or disputes
arising from such use.
All usage is at your own risk and discretion.

◆ Prompt
The full prompt for actual use is provided in the replies.
```

## Chibi Santa Character Figure Generation Prompt

> Minimal ürün fotoğrafı üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Faithfully reference the attached character image and reconstruct that character as a high-quality chibi character Santa Claus figure.
👤 Character Reproduction Rules (Most Important)
Strictly maintain the facial features, eye shape, hair color, hairstyle, and overall atmosphere of the original image.
The character must have a chibi physique of 2.5 to 3 heads tall (large head, short limbs, balanced proportions).
Although deformed, the figure must be recognizable at a glance.
🧸 Figure Texture and Modeling (Most Important)
PVC figure / finished toy-style texture.
Surface is smooth and uniform.
No excessive gloss, semi-matte to satin finish.
Edges are not too sharp, slightly rounded modeling.
Painting is careful with minimal unevenness, mass-production figure quality.
Cloth representation is sculpted clothing, not "real fabric."
* Do not include wool or fabric fiber texture.
* Be careful not to over-emphasize clay or resin texture.
🎄 Santa Costume (For Figures)
Santa costume (sculpted clothing) primarily in red.
White fur parts are expressed with molds.
Mini Santa hat (fixed modeling).
Boots and gloves appear integrated.
Decorations are minimal accents that enhance the figure's appearance, such as gold buttons and belt buckles.
🧍 Pose and Stand
Stable standing pose or light action pose.
Holding a present in one hand or waving.
Standing on a transparent or simple base.
Modeling balance is designed for mass production and stability (non-tipping).
🎨 Photography and Background
Product photography-style lighting.
Background is one of the following:
- White background
- Light gray
- Pale winter color
Product photo composition with the subject centered.
Shadows are soft, prioritizing cleanliness.
```

## High-Quality Industrial Product Photography with Functional Displacement

```
A set of calm, high-quality product photography images.

The main subject is a bottle of high-end nutritional supplement. The bottle is intact and clean, with the tamper-evident seal still in place, presenting a state of high precision and strict industrial control. The product is placed on a matte stone surface. The stone texture is rough, natural, and slightly aged, contrasting with the product's refinement.

The image features a clear detail of “functional displacement”: next to the supplement bottle, there is a miniature lounge chair with a clearly unreasonable scale, seemingly prepared for human rest but unusable, existing only symbolically. At the same time, a striking warning label is affixed to or near the bottle, with rational, cold text that fails to clearly state the source of the risk.

The overall composition is front-centered, stable, and restrained, without dramatic angles, emphasizing the product's presence and material details. Soft but directional studio lighting is used to clearly show the reflection on the bottle, the texture of the label paper, the details of the tamper-evident seal, and the subtle texture of the stone surface.

Miniature prop combination: random

#nanobanana  #NanoBananaPro  #prompt #IdeaGeneration
```

```
Role and Goal: You are a product photographer and a digital artist. Your job is to analyze this source image and reproduce it as a product photograph of a "Premium Laser Engraved Acrylic LED Stand." # Process 1. Analyze the source image to identify the [Main Subject] (e.g., person, pet, object) and the [Mood] (e.g., sentimental, energetic, calm). 2. Convert the visual style: - Convert the [Main Subject] into a purely glowing line art sketch (monochrome golden light, no original colors or fills used). - Create "Decorations": Artistic handwritten typography, doodles (e.g., hearts, stars, abstract shapes, speech bubbles), and decorative strokes surrounding the subject. These must be thematically consistent with the detected [Mood]. 3. Use the following template to compose the final product photo scene. # Image Generation Template: Macro photograph of a thick, die-cut, transparent acrylic stand light. Art: The acrylic surface features a laser-engraved, glowing golden line art of the [Detected Main Subject] from the source image. The engraving glows brightly with warm edge lighting. Base: A rustic wooden LED base supporting the acrylic. Setting: A luxurious, dimly lit indoor background, arranged with [Christmas decorative props: e.g., pine branches with red berries, glittering ornaments, warm fairy lights, pine cones, ribbons] to create a cozy, festive atmosphere. **Atmosphere: High contrast, photorealistic, 8K f/1.8 shallow depth of field, magical and emotional atmosphere. ## 3D Laser-Engraved Crystal Ornament Generator
```

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal ürün fotoğrafı üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
My Subject:  {argument name="subject name" default="Subject Name"}
Aspect Ratio: 9:16

# Role Definition:
You are an expert product photographer and creative art director specializing in high-end luxury goods. Your sole task is to generate a photorealistic image of a custom 3D subsurface laser-engraved crystal ornament based on a simple subject provided by the user.
# Core Directive:
The user will provide only the [Subject]. You must autonomously analyze this subject to determine the most aesthetically appropriate and enhancing configuration for the entire scene. You must decide on the crystal's shape, the base material, the surrounding environment, and the lighting mood to best complement the vibe of the user's subject.
# Creative Analysis Logic (Art Director Instructions):
Analyze the Vibe: Is the subject elegant (e.g., ballerina, swan), cozy (e.g., sleeping cat, family photo), powerful (e.g., lion, sports car), or architectural?
Determine Crystal Shape:
Elegant/Tall Subjects -> Use a tall, multi-faceted prism or diamond shape to enhance verticality.
Solid/Cozy Subjects -> Use a hefty, thick rectangular block or cube to convey weight and warmth.
Dynamic Subjects -> Use complex geometric shapes with many bevels to refract light dramatically.
Determine Environment & Lighting:
Elegant Vibe: Place it in a luxurious, dark, moody environment (art gallery, velvet study). Use dramatic, cool ambient light contrasting with warmth from the base.
Cozy/Warm Vibe: Place it in a homey, soft environment (wooden desk, near a fireplace). Use warm, soft, golden-hour lighting with cozy bokeh.
Modern/Tech Vibe: Place it on a sleek, minimalist surface (polished concrete, dark glass). Use clean, neutral lighting.
# Image Generation Template (Mandatory Technical Specs):
Whatever artistic choices you make based on the logic above, the final image MUST adhere to these technical specifications to ensure a realistic "3D Laser Engraving" look:
Engraving Style: The subject inside must NOT look like a solid 3D print. It MUST be described as a "volumetric white point-cloud engraving," having a "frosty, granular, subsurface texture" made of millions of micro-cracks inside the glass.
Lighting Interaction: The engraving must "glow brilliantly from within," illuminated entirely by warm, golden LED light originating from the base beneath it.
Material: The crystal must be heavy, clear, flawless K9 optical glass with beveled edges that catch and split the light.
Photography Style: Cinematic product photograph, macro lens, extremely shallow depth of field (bokeh), 8k resolution, hyper-detailed.
# Input Process:
I will now provide the subject. You will instantly apply the creative logic and generate the final image.
```

> Sinematik, lüks görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{argument name="brand name" default="[Brand Name]"}:  
A high-end, glossy conceptual art magazine editorial photograph, showcasing a unique, unexpected functional object conceptualized and designed by the brand.

**1. Concept and Object (AI Invention):**  
Based on the specified brand's design philosophy, heritage, and material vocabulary, the AI must invent a new type of practical product (not standard clothing, shoes, or bags). Examples may include household items, tech accessories, tools, or sports gear, reinterpreted through the brand's perspective. The object should feel sculptural yet functionally practical.

**2. Materials and Details (Ultra High-End):**  
The object is made of the brand's signature high-end, highly tactile materials (e.g., oxidized exotic leather, brushed aerospace-grade titanium, sculptural matte ceramic, molded carbon fiber, or technical haute couture textiles). Every detail is extremely realistic: visible stitching, microscopic material textures, precision engraving, and complex texture contrasts.

**3. Photography and Lighting (Cinematic Studio):**  
Shot using a medium format Phase One camera paired with a 100mm macro lens. Extremely shallow depth of field, with sharp focus on the object's details, and the background blurred into a creamy smoothness. Lighting is provided by exquisite studio softbox illumination: soft wraparound fill light, combined with precise contour lighting to highlight contours and material textures.

**4. Environment:**  
Seamless, flawless, pure ultra-light toned studio cyclorama background (e.g., desaturated mint green, light pink, or slightly yellowish white), shadowless.

**5. Layout and UI Elements (Strict Placement):**  
- **Bottom Right:** The brand's small, low-key monochrome gray logo.  
- **Bottom Left:** Small, extremely simple monochrome gray text describing the invented product. Font style similar to Manrope Regular, with extremely tight tracking (kerning) and balanced line spacing. Example format: "CONCEPT STUDY: [AI inserts invented product name]. MATERIAL: [AI inserts main material]. SS25."
```

```
  Matte finish on skin.
  Sharp focus with natural depth of field.
  Soft shadow beneath the figure as if placed on a flat surface.

colors: >
  Exact color reproduction based on the reference image.
  Background and graphic elements inferred to support readability of Japanese text.

text: >
  Product Name:
  「{{product_name}}」
  ※ The product name must be displayed and shown only once as a logo.

  Manufacturer Name:
  「{{manufacturer_name}}」
  ※ The manufacturer name must also be displayed and shown only once as a logo.

  Scale:
  1/7 Scale Figure

  Sculptor:
  {{manufacturer_name}}

  Price:
  The inferred retail price in Japanese Yen, based on the figure's scale, sculpting density, and paint expression.

  Size:
  The inferred total height in centimeters, based on the character's body type and 1/7 scale setting.

  Material:
  A material appropriate for a figure
```

## Museum-Quality Animal Portrait with Integrated Luxury Brand Pattern

> Minimal, lüks portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
MUSEUM-QUALITY ultra-detailed {argument name="animal name" default="[animal name]"} portrait, pure {argument name="background color" default="white/black"} background, professional studio photography, 8K resolution. This must be BREATHTAKINGLY BEAUTIFUL and ELEGANT.

PERFECT POSE - FULL BODY VISIBLE (CRITICAL):
- The {argument name="animal name" default="[animal name]"} is captured in graceful side profile view, facing left
- {argument name="pose description" default="[pose description: Standing pose for dogs/cats | Swimming pose for fish | Sitting pose for birds | Flying pose for butterflies]"}
- The COMPLETE animal is visible from head to tail/fins/wings to all legs/paws/feet
- Elegant posture - {argument name="elegant posture" default="[back straight for mammals | body curved for fish | wings spread for birds]"}, displaying perfect {argument name="breed/species" default="[breed/species]"} proportions
- Head held with dignity and grace
- The animal appears poised, noble, and regal like a fine art subject
- Overall silhouette shows complete graceful body lines in their most beautiful form

COMPOSITION (CRITICAL):
- FULL BODY of the animal is visible within the frame
- The animal occupies the central 60-70% of the image
- Generous {argument name="background color" default="background color"} negative space around all sides (top, bottom, left, right)
- Animal is centered with balanced spacing
- Clean, minimalist, gallery-worthy composition
- High-end luxury brand campaign aesthetic

BASE COLOR/TEXTURE:
{argument name="base description based on animal" default="[base description based on animal]"}
For spotted animals (Dalmatian, Leopard, Koi fish): 
  - Pristine {argument name="base color" default="white/golden/orange"} base with natural {argument name="texture" default="texture: fur/scales/feathers"}
  - The base color is luminous and clean
  
For solid color animals (Black cat, White dove, Golden retriever):
  - {argument name="base color" default="[Base color]"} coat/fur/feathers with silky sheen
  - Natural highlights defining body structure

BRAND PATTERN INTEGRATION - {argument name="brand name" default="[brand name]"} (CLEAR AND RECOGNIZABLE):

PRIMARY (55-60% - Natural animal patterns):
- Natural {argument name="natural pattern" default="spots/stripes/scales/feathers"} in organic distribution
- Maintains the authentic look of the {argument name="animal species" default="[animal species]"}
- Irregular, asymmetric patterns true to nature

SECONDARY (40-45% - CLEAR Brand Elements):
The {argument name="natural pattern elements" default="natural pattern elements"} are strategically arranged and shaped to clearly form recognizable {argument name="brand name" default="[brand name]"} motifs:

Brand: {argument name="brand" default="[Louis Vuitton]"}
Core visual elements: {argument name="core visual elements" default="[LV monogram letters, quatrefoil four-petal flowers, geometric diamonds]"}

On the MAIN BODY (most visible area - torso/back/side):
- Multiple clear "{argument name="core logo" default="[core logo]"}" formed by connected {argument name="pattern connection" default="spots/stripes/markings"} - making them easily readable and recognizable
- Several {argument name="brand iconic pattern" default="[brand iconic pattern]"} clearly visible - {argument name="example" default="[example: quatrefoil flower shapes formed by 4 rounded spots in clover pattern]"}
- {argument name="decorative geometric pattern" default="[decorative geometric pattern]"} between the main logos

On the HEAD and NECK area:
- 1-2 smaller {argument name="logo" default="[Logo]"} formations  
- {argument name="iconic pattern" default="[iconic pattern]"} near the {argument name="facial features" default="ear/eye/facial features"}

On the LEGS/TAIL/WINGS/FINS:
- {argument name="geometric pattern or repeating motif" default="[geometric pattern or repeating motif]"} suggesting the brand's signature pattern

Pattern execution requirements:
- The {argument name="brand core elements" default="[brand core elements]"} should be CLEARLY RECOGNIZABLE at first glance - not abstract hints
- {argument name="pattern elements" default="[Pattern elements]"} are arranged in organized repeating pattern like {argument name="brand" default="[brand]"} signature print/canvas
```

## Two-Phase Prompt for Absurd Product Fusion Concepts

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
PHASE 1 PROMPT: Analyze these [{argument name="object 1" default="OBJECT 1"}], [{argument name="object 2" default="OBJECT 2"}], [{argument name="object 3" default="OBJECT 3"}] images. Extract: 1) Primary function, 2) Material DNA (metal/plastic ratio, texture), 3) Ergonomic signature (grip angle, weight distribution), 4) Mechanical principle, 5) Cultural association. Output as structured JSON.

PHASE 2 PROMPT: Create a 3x3 grid of photorealistic product renderings on a pristine white background. Each cell must be a hyper-realistic 3D concept that fuses elements from the previous analysis. For each invention, add minimalist navy blue text in Helvetica Neue: [Invention Name], [Core Mechanism], [Use Case]. Style: Dieter Rams meets Dyson - clean, technical, premium. Include subtle shadows and a 1px gold border on each cell. Make inventions absurd yet plausible (e.g., "Acoustic Peeler: uses sound waves to strip vegetables").
```

## Nano Banana Pro Original Snack Package Generation Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-realistic live-action photo of a hand holding a snack package. The background also shows shelves lined with different flavors and types of packages featuring the character from the reference image.

Character Name: "{argument name="Character Name" default="〇〇"}" (If not specified, strongly parody the characteristics of the reference image character with intense humor)

Snack Setting: "{argument name="Snack Setting" default="〇〇"}"

If not specified, randomly select one type from all snack genres sold in convenience stores, supermarkets, or candy stores, and boost the design for either eccentricity or stylishness.

Realistic photo-style depiction of an original snack package fully decorated with the reference image character, held in a hand. The design should be natural enough to look plausible on a store shelf. The surrounding area of the snack should realistically show the store interior.

Snack Package Design:

Place the reference image character inside the package.
Emphasize the name of the snack with intense humor based on the characteristics of the reference image.
The package should be ultra-realistic, high-quality, and a highly stylish design that stimulates appetite.

Realistically reproduce gloss reflections and material texture.
Include an appetizing photo of the snack contents.
Parts other than decoration should be transparent vinyl, or have a transparent film window allowing the contents to be partially visible.

Place multiple humorous and exaggerated catchphrases and product names based on the reference image, using a font that reflects the atmosphere of the reference image, prominently.

Draw a background (sales floor) appropriate for the snack's worldview.
Also, ensure emotional and chromatic consistency between the background and the package concept.

📦 Package Shape Randomizer (Snack Package Shape Randomizer)

Regardless of the snack type, randomly select from the following package shapes with equal probability.
Furthermore, ensure the shape, background, texture, and light source are consistent.

◆ Shape Category List (Total 12 types)

Vertical bag package (Snack type)

Horizontal bag package (Chocolate bar/Candy bar type)

Box type (Small box) (Candy/Ramune type)

Box type (Large box) (Family pack type)

Blister pack (Gum/Gummy/with bonus item)

Cup container (Potato chips/Ice cream style)

Cylindrical container (Tube candy/Tower potato chips)

Stick type packaging (Long, thin candy type)

Jelly suction pouch (Drinkable gummy/Juice type)

Booklet type (Opens to reveal stickers or cards)

Toy integrated package (Built-in toy)

Eccentric shaped package
	Example: Character face 3D shape, car shape, treasure chest shape, glowing bottle shape, etc.

🧭 Scene Optimization (Scene Optimization Rules)

Automatically adjust the following to create an environment where contrast stands out and the package is highlighted, according to the snack package design and concept:

Lighting (Brightness, warm/cool colors, direction)
Seasonality (Summer/Winter, Spring/Autumn)
Indoor/Outdoor
Time of day (Day/Night)
```

## Layered Acrylic Artwork Product Photography

> Ürün fotoğrafı üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Product photography, perfectly straight-on frontal view. CRITICAL: The original art style, linework, and character features of the input illustration MUST BE STRICTLY PRESERVED. Do not abstract or simplify the drawing itself.

The artwork is a physical multi-layered construction mounted on a white wall.
**COMPOSITION RULE:**
1. The aspect ratio and orientation of the final product photography should match the dimensions of the input source image.
2. A large clear base panel is secured with visible metallic standoffs.
3. Crucially, the entire die-cut illustration (all layers) MUST be contained entirely within the boundaries of this base panel.
4. There should be an appropriate clear margin between the edges of the illustration and the edges of the base panel.

**MATERIAL PHYSICS RULE (HIGH TRANSMISSION):**
On top of the base, the illustration is reconstructed using **ultra-clear, highly luminous tinted acrylic sheets** designed for maximal light transmission.
1. The colors are the color of the transparent material itself (glass-like), not painted.
2. **For Skin/White Areas:** MUST be **pure, colorless, ultra-clear glass-like acrylic**.
3. **For Colored Areas (Hair/Clothes):** Use **highly translucent colored acrylic**. The color is vibrant but extremely see-through, allowing significant light to pass.

**LIGHTING & SHADOWS:**
Strong cool diagonal lighting passes through the highly transparent artwork. **Instead of creating dark or heavy shadows, the light casts bright, glowing, highly translucent colorful caustics onto the wall, showing the wall's texture underneath.** The shadow area should feel light and airy, revealing the original colors as bright projections. 8k resolution, ultra-detailed.
```

## Chibi RPG Game Campaign Key Visual

> Afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[Game Campaign]
- Game Title: {argument name="game title" default="Treasure Hunt!"}
- Game Overview/Features: {argument name="description" default="Find treasures on a great adventure island!"}
- Genre/Subtext: {argument name="subtext" default="RPG Adventure Action!"}

[Quality, Production, Composition]
- Style: Illustration for event announcements for Japanese mobile RPGs or casual games. A colorful and joyful fantasy world.
- Subject: Adorable chibi characters (deformed heroes or mascots) opening a treasure chest with smiles. Golden light and magical particles are overflowing dazzlingly from the chest.
- Background: Blue sky, white clouds, and islands or forests in a lush fantasy world.
- Typography (for GPT-image / Nano Banana Pro): A fantasy-style decorative logotype of {argument name="game title" default="Treasure Hunt!"} is designed in the center or top of the screen, with {argument name="description" default="Find treasures on a great adventure island!"} and {argument name="subtext" default="RPG Adventure Action!"} added in an easy-to-read layout.
- Color/Lighting: Bright, high-saturation fantasy colors, magical gloss, and soft shadow depiction.

[Strict Layout and Output Constraints (Required)]
- Output the finished design itself, filling the entire screen. Backgrounds and scene descriptions (walls, spaces, shadows, etc.) within the design may follow the instructions in the main text.
- Prohibitions: Photos of the finished poster in a frame, photos pinned to a wall, mockup photos placed on a desk or paper, perspective distortion of paper edges, or drop shadows.
- Output the finished flat 2D design itself, filling the entire canvas. Scene elements inside the design (walls, rooms, shadows) described above are allowed. Absolutely NO photo-of-a-poster mockups: no picture frames, no poster-on-wall or poster-on-desk shots, no perspective warp or drop shadow around the artwork's edges.

- Aspect Ratio: --ar 16:9
```

## Expressive Painterly Realistic Hybrid Portrait

> Sinematik, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Using the attached object, recreate it as identically as possible. Create a sophisticated hybrid portrait blending expressive painterly realism with polished cinematic digital illustration.

Preserve the exact facial structure, expression, pose, hairstyle, clothing, proportions, and overall composition. Use visible, confident brushstrokes, subtle dry-brush textures, delicate sketch lines, soft glazing, natural pigment variation, and handcrafted imperfections—avoid an overly smooth or airbrushed look.

Use warm peach, coral, terracotta, amber, and soft-pink skin tones with rich shadows in mauve, plum, charcoal, espresso, and slate. Add realistic luminous highlights to the forehead, nose, cheekbones, and lips.

Make the eyes highly expressive and cinematic, with detailed irises, realistic reflections, fine lashes, and controlled catchlights. Keep facial anatomy convincing, elegant, and mature.

Hair and clothing should have fluid painterly strokes, natural folds, selective sharp details, and loose expressive edges. Use dramatic directional cinematic lighting, subtle rim light, atmospheric shadows, and soft background blending.

Overall style: premium contemporary concept art combining traditional hand-painted texture, impressionistic realism, elegant linework, cinematic polish, and refined character illustration.

Minimal atmospheric background, sophisticated warm-neutral palette, strong focus on the face and gaze.

Aspect ratio: 9:16 vertical.
```

## Artifact Diorama SQL Generation


## Woodpunk Kinetic Sculpture Automaton

> Ürün fotoğrafı üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
1. The Semantic Inference Engine (Dynamic Material Mapping):  
Input A is ANY Topic (Object, Animal, Plant, or Concept).
Analyze the subject's inherent properties and map them to Artisanal Materials:
The Primary Body (The Chassis): Infer the subject's main color/weight and assign a corresponding Hardwood. 
The Accents (The Inlay): Infer the subject's patterns or details and assign a contrasting Marquetry Material. 
The Mechanics (The Skeleton): Infer the subject's joints or power source and assign Metal Hardware.
2. The "Immutable" Container (The Automaton):
Goal: A "Da Vinci Workshop" Product Shot.
The Object: The subject is rendered as a complex, freestanding Wooden Mechanical Toy.
The Base: The model stands on a hand-carved wooden block representing its semantic environment.
The Scale: Macro photography of a tabletop object (approx. 12 inches tall).
3. The Clockwork Interior (The Transformation):
The Cutaway: A section of the outer "wood shell" is visually removed or hinged open.
The Guts: Inside, the biological or mechanical inner workings are replaced by Clockwork Mechanics.
Rule: Visible gears, bicycle chains, watch springs, and brass flywheels must imply the object moves via wind-up power.
The Fasteners: Tiny brass screws, rivets, and hinges are visible along the seams of the wood.
4. The Narrative Figure (The Operator):
The Character: A standard Jointed Wooden Mannequin (Artist's Doll style) is integrated into the scene.
The Role: The figure acts as the maintainer, rider, or operator of the subject.
Interaction: They are climbing a ladder to polish the object, turning a giant wind-up key, or steering the beast/machine.
The Accessories: The doll wears carved wooden gear appropriate to the context.
5. Lighting & Atmosphere:
Lighting: Softbox Studio Lighting. Diffused, high-end commercial lighting that creates long, elegant highlights on the varnished curves.
Background: Neutral Studio Grey. Seamless infinity backdrop to focus entirely on the material textures.
Texture: High Gloss Varnish. The wood must look expensive, smooth, and deeply saturated.
Output: ONE image, 1:1 Aspect Ratio, Product Photography, "Woodpunk" aesthetic, 8k Resolution.
```

> Sinematik, fütüristik illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a breathtaking, ultra-premium anime cinematic artwork set in a dark fantasy world, designed to look like a frame from the most expensive anime ever produced.

{argument name="warrior" default="A calm, impossibly charismatic male warrior"} stands alone on the edge of a massive ruined temple overlooking a futuristic ancient city at night. He has sharp expressive eyes, detailed black hair moving naturally in the wind, an elegant dark combat coat with intricate silver detailing, subtle battle damage, and an effortless, intimidating posture.

He is not attacking or shouting. He simply stands completely still, looking slightly downward with an emotionless, confident expression. His presence alone dominates the entire scene.

Around him is an {argument name="aura" default="enormous aura field—deep atmospheric energy"} flowing naturally through the environment like invisible pressure made visible. Thin luminous particles, drifting embers, fragments of dust and rain curve around his body. The ground beneath his feet shows subtle cracks from the sheer pressure of his presence. His coat and hair move dramatically in the energy-filled wind.

Behind him, enormous storm clouds spiral across the sky, illuminated by distant lightning. A {argument name="moon" default="gigantic moon"} breaks through the clouds, creating a powerful silhouette around his figure. Far below, thousands of tiny city lights create immense depth and scale.

The aura should feel beautiful, intimidating and otherworldly, not like a generic glowing outline. Use layered translucent energy, atmospheric distortion, subtle light refraction, floating particles and faint wave-like pressure around the character. Keep the character's face and body sharply defined while the environment reacts to his presence.

Composition: low-angle hero shot, character positioned slightly off-center, massive negative space above him, dramatic depth, foreground particles, distant city below, enormous sky, cinematic perspective.

Visual style: elite modern anime production, extraordinarily detailed linework, painterly backgrounds, realistic lighting, physically believable shadows, cinematic color grading, realistic atmospheric perspective, highly detailed eyes, hair strands and fabric, subtle skin texture, volumetric moonlight, high contrast, razor-sharp focal subject, breathtaking environmental detail.

Blend stylized anime character design with realistic cinematic rendering, so the image feels like a premium anime still rather than a generic illustration.

The character should radiate the ultimate “aura farming” energy: silent confidence, overwhelming presence, effortless dominance, mysterious power, elegance and danger.

No text, no logos, no watermark, no goofy expression, no exaggerated screaming, no giant muscles, no generic superhero costume, no messy composition, no excessive neon, no blurry face, no distorted anatomy, no extra fingers, no deformed hands, no cheap CGI appearance.

Aspect ratio: 16:9. Ultra-detailed, cinematic, breathtaking, premium anime key visual
```

## Nendoroid Style Manga Diorama

> Minimal çizgi roman karesi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
<instruction>
  1.  Semantic Inference Engine  
Input A is a {argument name="character" default="Manga/Anime Character or Comic Book Hero"}.
Deconstruct the character to generate   3 Action Assets  :
     The Power (The Effect):  Identify the signature attack.
         (e.g., Fireball, Web-Slinging, Psychic Blast, Lightning). 
     The Sound (The Onomatopoeia):  Identify the comic book sound effect text.
         (e.g., "BOOM!", "SMASH!", "THWIP!", "KABOOM!"). 
     The Color (The Aura):  Identify the primary energy color.
         (e.g., Orange, Red, Purple, Green). 
  2. Container (The Display Stand):  
Goal: "Nendoroid" Style Diorama Photography.
     The Stage:  A simple   L-Shaped Display Base   (Vertical Back wall + Horizontal Floor).
     The Backdrop:  The vertical wall is covered in a collage of   Black & White Manga Panels   or Comic Strips, creating a chaotic, ink-heavy background.
  3. The Figure (The Chibi Hero):  
     The Style:    Chibi / Super-Deformed (SD).   Large head, small body, big expressive eyes.
     The Pose:  Dynamic, mid-air action pose (punching towards the camera or casting a spell).
     The Material:  Smooth, matte   PVC or Vinyl Plastic   (collectible toy aesthetic).
  4. The Visual FX (The 2.5D Impact):  
     The Text:  The "Sound" (Step 1) is rendered as large, jagged, colorful   3D Text Plates   (Orange/Red with black outlines) floating in the air around the character.
     The Debris:  3D shards of rock, glass, or torn paper fly outward from the center, simulating an explosion.
     The Energy:  Translucent, colored plastic parts (representing the "Power") swirl around the figure's hands or feet.
  5. Lighting & Atmosphere:  
     Lighting:    Bright Studio Key Light.   Even, shadowless illumination typical of toy photography.
     Vibe:  Energetic, playful, and explosive.
  Output:   ONE image, 1:1 Aspect Ratio, 3D Render, "Anime Figure" aesthetic, High Saturation.
</instruction>
```

```
Transform the uploaded person into a premium original {argument name="color scheme" default="black-and-crimson"} futuristic {argument name="theme" default="arachnid-inspired"} character in a cinematic gaming-room environment while preserving the person’s natural appearance and recognizable identity.

IDENTITY LOCK

CHARACTER DESIGN

POSE

CAMERA
High-angle cinematic perspective looking downward. Full-body framing with enough surrounding environment to establish the gaming room. Natural lens perspective. Realistic depth of field. Subject remains the primary focal point.
LIGHTING & STYLE
Soft cinematic key lighting. Deep controlled shadows. Subtle crimson ambient illumination. Natural skin tones. Gentle rim lighting. Realistic fabric reflections. Soft volumetric atmosphere. Premium editorial color grading. Photorealistic fashion photography. Extremely detailed textures. Sharp facial detail. Realistic materials. High-end cinematic gaming campaign aesthetic. ## Cyberpunk Futuristic Visor Portrait
```

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Hyper-realistic 3D rendered digital art portrait in a 9:16 vertical format. Subject is a {argument name="subject" default="platinum blonde/white hair pulled back"}, wearing a {argument name="accessory" default="sleek matte black wraparound futuristic visor with a glowing amber-orange LED neon strip"} running along its inner bottom edge. The visor sits flush against the face with no gap.

**Camera Angle & Composition**

Camera angle is an extreme low-angle (worm's-eye view), with the viewer looking upward at the subject. The subject's face is tilted upward and slightly to the right in a 3/4 profile, gazing toward something above the frame. Lips are slightly parted, jaw relaxed, and the expression conveys deep immersion, awe, and transcendence. The neck is fully elongated and exposed. The subject wears a black turtleneck that disappears seamlessly into the background.

**Lighting**

Extreme low-key, high-contrast cinematic lighting with an absolute pure black background and zero ambient spill.

The {argument name="primary light" default="primary light source is the glowing visor itself"}, casting intense amber-orange underlighting across the chin, lips, cheeks, nose bridge, and neck from below.

A secondary cool cyan-teal ambient light wraps around the top of the head, forehead, and upper cheek from above-left.

The lighting creates a dramatic two-tone color temperature clash between warm burnt orange/gold and cool electric cyan/teal. Subsurface scattering is visible on the nose tip and cheek edges where the orange light passes through the skin. Realistic light bloom and a soft orange corona radiate from the LED strip.

**Color Palette**

Absolute black, electric cyan, burnt amber-orange, and warm gold.

No whites. No pastel colors. No environmental detail.

**Style**

Dark sci-fi cyberpunk concept art inspired by the Blade Runner 2049 cinematic aesthetic. High-end 3D render featuring displacement-mapped skin texture, subsurface scattering, hard shadow terminator lines, realistic light bloom, and an ascending aspirational composition. Film poster and AAA game cinematic quality with a brooding atmospheric mood and futuristic technology immersion theme. Ultra-high detail, dramatic, and minimal.
```

```
Use the uploaded image as the exact visual composition, lighting reference, pose inspiration, and cinematic atmosphere. Create an ultra-realistic cinematic {argument name="theme" default="fantasy portrait"} of a young man standing in a dark cosmic void filled with floating particles, subtle stars, glowing mist, and ethereal energy trails. The subject is shown from a low-angle medium close-up perspective with one hand raised toward the camera, fingers extended naturally as if controlling mysterious cosmic energy. Around the raised hand, multiple luminous {argument name="glow color" default="white"} glowing rings float in the air, emitting soft radiant light, electric energy strands, smoke-like plasma trails, and tiny sparkling particles. The rings appear semi-transparent, magical, and futuristic, creating a celestial sci-fi aura.

The subject has thick slightly messy dark hair with loose strands falling naturally over the forehead, soft expressive eyes looking upward toward the glowing rings, and a calm, mesmerized facial expression. He wears a translucent {argument name="clothing item" default="wet white shirt"} with realistic fabric texture, subtle folds, glossy moisture reflections, and partially visible skin underneath, adding dramatic cinematic realism. A thin silver chain necklace is faintly visible around the neck.

Lighting is highly cinematic and moody: the glowing rings act as the primary light source, casting soft white highlights and subtle rim lighting across the face, hand, hair, and wet shirt. Add realistic volumetric fog, atmospheric haze, depth-of-field blur, soft bloom, lens diffusion, and high dynamic range contrast. The background should remain deep black with floating light particles and swirling energy effects to enhance the mystical atmosphere.

Style: ultra-realistic, cinematic fantasy, sci-fi celestial aesthetic, hyper detailed skin texture, realistic eyes, sharp facial details, dramatic contrast, soft glow effects, high-end movie still quality, Unreal Engine 5 realism, shallow depth of field, 8K detail, vertical portrait composition.
```
